急性 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 基孔肯雅病毒 感染患者血清抗体反应和免疫状况的比较分析

Comparative analysis of serum antibody responses and immune landscapes in patients with acute DENV-1, DENV-2, and Chikungunya virus infections
作者： Ying Zhang, Sheng-Ze Zhang, Long-Yu Zou, Hao-Lu Zha, Wei-Jian Tian, Peng-Jie Ma, Ya-Chao Zuo, Cai-Jun Sun, Yue-Long Shu, Jian-Hui Yuan
摘要中文翻译简介 登革热病毒 (DENV) 和 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 是主要的蚊媒虫媒病毒,它们越来越多地在热带和亚热带地区(包括中国南部)共同传播。然而,这些病毒在多大程度上诱导共享或不同的 宿主 免疫应答 仍然知之甚少。本研究旨在比较急性 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 CHIKV 感染期间 免疫应答 的特征。材料与方法 总共分析了 80 份血清样本,其中 65 份来自实验室确诊的 DENV-1 (n = 29)、DENV-2 (n = 10) 或 CHIKV (n = 26) 感染患者和 15 名健康对照。通过实时逆转录聚合酶链反应检测病毒 RNA。通过酶联免疫吸附测定法测量病毒特异性 IgM 和 IgG 反应,并使用多重测定法对血清细胞因子进行定量。组间比较使用非参数检验,并使用 Spearman 等级相关性评估抗体反应、细胞因子浓度和病毒 RNA 水平之间的关联。使用 Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) 程序调整多重比较。结果三种感染具有干扰素相关免疫激活和相对抑制的全身炎症的共同特征,但表现出不同的免疫介质特征,其特征是不同的炎症介质和生长因子反应。经过多重测试校正后,细胞因子-细胞因子相关性分析揭示了免疫介质共变的共享模式和感染特异性模式,所有三个感染组中的共享关联涉及 IL-9、TNF-β、CCL4 和 CXCL1。在感染过程中观察到病原体特异性 IgM 反应与 Ct 值之间的正相关性,尽管经过多重测试校正后,DENV-2 的相关性并不具有统计学意义。IgG 反应仅在 DENV-1 感染中显示出类似的关联。细胞因子和抗体谱的整合进一步揭示了不同的免疫关联模式:DENV-1 显示出更广泛的关联,涉及病毒 RNA 水平、炎症介质和体液反应,而 CHIKV 显示出更有限的模式,集中在炎症介质和 IgM 反应之间的关联。讨论 这些发现揭示了急性 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 CHIKV 感染期间共有和不同的 免疫应答,并为研究急性虫媒病毒感染期间病毒相关的免疫调节提供了比较框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热病毒(DENV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)是主要的蚊媒虫媒病毒,它们越来越多地在热带和亚热带地区(包括中国南部)共同传播。然而,这些病毒在多大程度上诱导共享或不同的 宿主 免疫应答 仍然知之甚少。
设计与对象
本研究旨在比较急性 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 CHIKV 感染期间 免疫应答 的特征。总共分析了 80 份血清样本,其中 65 份来自实验室确诊的 DENV-1 (n = 29)、DENV-2 (n = 10) 或 CHIKV (n = 26) 感染患者和 15 名健康对照者。
核心方法
通过酶联免疫吸附测定法测量病毒特异性 IgM 和 IgG 反应,并使用多重测定法对血清细胞因子进行定量。组间比较使用非参数检验,并使用 Spearman 等级相关性评估抗体反应、细胞因子浓度和病毒 RNA 水平之间的关联。
主要结果
这三种感染具有干扰素相关免疫激活和相对抑制的全身炎症的共同特征,但表现出不同的免疫介质特征,其特征是不同的炎症介质和生长因子反应。经过多重测试校正后,细胞因子-细胞因子相关性分析揭示了免疫介质共变的共享模式和感染特异性模式,所有三个感染组中的共享关联涉及 IL-9、TNF-β、CCL4 和 CXCL1。
解释与创新
这些发现揭示了急性 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 CHIKV 感染期间共有和不同的 免疫应答,并为研究急性虫媒病毒感染期间病毒相关的免疫调节提供了比较框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为研究急性虫媒病毒感染期间病毒相关的免疫调节提供了一个比较框架,这可以为未来对 免疫应答 的研究提供信息,并有可能改善这些感染的诊断和治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其重点关注急性感染患者的血清样本,这可能无法完全代表其他情况下的免疫动态。证据是中等的,因为它基于仅抽象的分析。

Comparative analysis of serum antibody responses and immune landscapes in patients with acute DENV-1, DENV-2, and Chikungunya virus infections

Authors: Ying Zhang, Sheng-Ze Zhang, Long-Yu Zou, Hao-Lu Zha, Wei-Jian Tian, Peng-Jie Ma, Ya-Chao Zuo, Cai-Jun Sun, Yue-Long Shu, Jian-Hui Yuan
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) are major mosquito-borne arboviruses that increasingly co-circulate in tropical and subtropical regions, including southern China. However, the extent to which these viruses induce shared or distinct host immune responses remains poorly understood. This study aimed to comparatively characterize immune responses during acute DENV-1, DENV-2, and CHIKV infections. Materials and methods A total of 80 serum samples were analyzed, including 65 samples from patients with laboratory-confirmed DENV-1 (n = 29), DENV-2 (n = 10), or CHIKV (n = 26) infections and 15 healthy controls.Viral RNA was detected by real-time reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction. Virus-specific IgM and IgG responses were measured by Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay, and serum cytokines were quantified using a multiplex assay. Group comparisons used nonparametric tests, and associations among antibody responses, cytokine concentrations, and viral RNA levels were assessed using Spearman’s rank correlation. Multiple comparisons were adjusted using the Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) procedure. Results The three infections shared features of interferon-associated immune activation and relatively restrained systemic inflammation but exhibited distinct immune mediator profiles, characterized by differential inflammatory mediator and growth factor responses. After multiple-testing correction, cytokine–cytokine correlation analysis revealed both shared and infection-specific patterns of immune mediator covariation, with shared associations involving IL-9, TNF-β, CCL4, and CXCL1 across all three infection groups. Positive associations between pathogen-specific IgM responses and Ct values were observed across the infections, although the association was not statistically significant for DENV-2 after multiple-testing correction. IgG responses showed a similar association only in DENV-1 infection. Integration of cytokine and antibody profiles further revealed distinct immune association patterns: DENV-1 showed broader associations involving viral RNA levels, inflammatory mediators, and humoral responses, whereas CHIKV displayed a more restricted pattern centered on associations between inflammatory mediators and IgM responses. Discussion These findings reveal shared and distinct immune responses during acute DENV-1, DENV-2, and CHIKV infections and provide a comparative framework for investigating virus-associated immune regulation during acute arboviral infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) are major mosquito-borne arboviruses that increasingly co-circulate in tropical and subtropical regions, including southern China. However, the extent to which these viruses induce shared or distinct host immune responses remains poorly understood.
Design and population
This study aimed to comparatively characterize immune responses during acute DENV-1, DENV-2, and CHIKV infections. A total of 80 serum samples were analyzed, including 65 samples from patients with laboratory-confirmed DENV-1 (n = 29), DENV-2 (n = 10), or CHIKV (n = 26) infections and 15 healthy controls.
Core methods
Virus-specific IgM and IgG responses were measured by Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay, and serum cytokines were quantified using a multiplex assay. Group comparisons used nonparametric tests, and associations among antibody responses, cytokine concentrations, and viral RNA levels were assessed using Spearman’s rank correlation.
Main results
The three infections shared features of interferon-associated immune activation and relatively restrained systemic inflammation but exhibited distinct immune mediator profiles, characterized by differential inflammatory mediator and growth factor responses. After multiple-testing correction, cytokine–cytokine correlation analysis revealed both shared and infection-specific patterns of immune mediator covariation, with shared associations involving IL-9, TNF-β, CCL4, and CXCL1 across all three infection groups.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings reveal shared and distinct immune responses during acute DENV-1, DENV-2, and CHIKV infections and provide a comparative framework for investigating virus-associated immune regulation during acute arboviral infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a comparative framework for investigating virus-associated immune regulation during acute arboviral infections, which can inform future studies on immune responses and potentially improve diagnostic and therapeutic strategies for these infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on serum samples from patients with acute infections, which may not fully represent the immune dynamics in other contexts. The evidence is moderate as it is based on an abstract-only analysis.

用于基孔肯雅热 IgM ELISA 的 EQA 血清样品的均质性和稳定性评估

Homogeneity and stability evaluation of EQA serum samples for Chikungunya IgM ELISA
作者： Himabindhu Srinivasamurthy, Shashiraja Padukone, Chethan Jayaram, Dinesh Reghunathan, Harmanmeet Kaur, Labanya Mukhopadhyay, Nivedita Gupta, Shantala Gowdara Basawarajappa
摘要中文翻译PT 提供商 (PTP) 参与实验室的地理分布可能会导致分发 PT 面板时的运输延迟,这可能会导致由于可疑的样品降解而取消 PT 面板的资格,从而需要昂贵的面板重新分发。这项研究通过评估模拟真实运输条件的不同温度和时间段的均匀性和稳定性来评估面板质量。结果根据 ISO 13528:2022 进行分析。这项研究表明,PT 组中的 CHIKV 阳性样本在所有测试的温度范围和时间段内都保持稳健,在长达 168 小时和最高温度 45 °C 下保持 100% 定性一致性。数据可用性 当前研究期间没有生成或分析数据集。2026 年 3 月 30 日 - Andrew A、Navien TN、Yeoh TS、Citartan M、Mangantig E、Sum MSH 等人 (2022) 诊断 感染的 诊断 血清学检测的诊断准确性:系统评价和荟萃分析。Biochem Med 27(1):37–48 致谢 作者衷心感谢印度医学研究委员会卫生研究部提供的资金以及班加罗尔医学院和研究所管理部门为促进本研究提供的支持。摘要 基孔肯雅热是一种由 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 引起的蚊媒病毒性疾病,通过伊蚊叮咬感染人类。蚊子,引起高烧。诊断 包括通过 ELISA 检测针对 CHIKV 的 IgM 抗体。卫生研究部 (DHR) — 印度医学研究委员会 (ICMR) 通过能力验证 (PT) 实施了外部质量保证计划 (EQAS),以确保 CHIKV-IgM-ELISA 检测的准确性和可靠性...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估用于基孔肯雅热 IgM ELISA 的外部质量保证 (EQA) 血清样品的均质性和稳定性,以解决运输过程中样品降解的挑战,这可能会影响诊断准确性。
设计与对象
该研究由卫生研究部 (DHR) — 印度医学研究委员会 (ICMR) 通过外部质量保证计划 (EQAS) 进行,使用分发给印度实验室网络的能力验证 (PT) 小组。参与实验室在模拟真实运输条件下测试了 CHIKV 阳性样本。
核心方法
通过模拟真实运输条件的各种温度范围和时间段来评估 EQA 血清样品的均匀性和稳定性。评估遵循 ISO 13528:2022 中概述的指南。
主要结果
PT 面板中的 CHIKV 阳性样品在所有测试温度范围和时间段内均保持稳定,在最高温度 45 °C 下长达 168 小时保持 100% 定性一致性。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为,如果在 168 小时内接收且最高运输温度为 45 °C,PT 面板可以可靠地用于基孔肯雅 IgM ELISA 测试的 EQA,这是确保现场条件下此类测试可靠性的新发现。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为在基孔肯雅热 IgM ELISA 检测中可靠使用 EQA 血清样本提供了具体指导,可以为质量保证计划的设计提供信息,并降低因运输过程中降解而导致样本不合格的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖剩余的、去识别化的样本而受到限制,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。证据是强有力的,因为它基于模拟条件下的直接测试并根据 ISO 13528:2022 标准进行分析。

Homogeneity and stability evaluation of EQA serum samples for Chikungunya IgM ELISA

Authors: Himabindhu Srinivasamurthy, Shashiraja Padukone, Chethan Jayaram, Dinesh Reghunathan, Harmanmeet Kaur, Labanya Mukhopadhyay, Nivedita Gupta, Shantala Gowdara Basawarajappa
Original abstract (English)
The geographical distribution of the participant laboratories from the PT provider (PTP) can lead to delayed transportation while distributing the PT panels, which could result in disqualification of PT panels due to suspected sample degradation and thus necessitates expensive panel redistributions. This study evaluated panel quality by assessing homogeneity and stability across various temperatures and time periods that simulate real-world transit conditions. The results were analyzed in accordance with ISO 13528:2022. This study shows that the CHIKV-positive samples in the PT panel remained robust across all tested temperature ranges and time periods, maintaining 100% qualitative concordance for up to 168 h and at a maximum temperature of 45 °C. Data availability No datasets were generated or analyzed during the current study. March 30, 2026 - Andrew A, Navien TN, Yeoh TS, Citartan M, Mangantig E, Sum MSH et al (2022) Diagnostic accuracy of serological tests for the diagnosis of Chikungunya virus infection: a systematic review and meta-analysis. Biochem Med 27(1):37–48 Acknowledgments The authors sincerely acknowledge Department of Health Research—Indian Council of Medical Research for funding and the administration of Bangalore Medical College & Research Institute for facilitating the study. Abstract Chikungunya, a mosquito-borne viral disease caused by Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), infects humans through the bite of Aedes sp. mosquitoes, causing high fever. Diagnosis includes the detection of IgM antibodies against CHIKV by ELISA. Department of Health Research (DHR)—Indian Council of Medical Research (ICMR) implemented an External Quality Assurance Scheme (EQAS) through Proficiency Testing (PT) to ensure the accuracy and reliability of CHIKV-IgM-ELISA testing across a...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the homogeneity and stability of external quality assurance (EQA) serum samples for Chikungunya IgM ELISA to address the challenge of sample degradation during transit, which could affect diagnostic accuracy.
Design and population
The study was conducted by the Department of Health Research (DHR)—Indian Council of Medical Research (ICMR) through an External Quality Assurance Scheme (EQAS) using proficiency testing (PT) panels distributed to a network of laboratories in India. The participant laboratories were involved in testing the CHIKV-positive samples under simulated real-world transit conditions.
Core methods
The homogeneity and stability of the EQA serum samples were assessed by simulating various temperature ranges and time periods that mimic real-world transportation conditions. The evaluation followed the guidelines outlined in ISO 13528:2022.
Main results
The CHIKV-positive samples in the PT panel remained robust across all tested temperature ranges and time periods, maintaining 100% qualitative concordance for up to 168 h and at a maximum temperature of 45 °C.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as demonstrating that the PT panels can be reliably used for EQA of Chikungunya IgM ELISA tests if received within 168 h and under a maximum transit temperature of 45 °C, which is a novel finding for ensuring the reliability of such testing in field conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide specific guidance for the reliable use of EQA serum samples in Chikungunya IgM ELISA testing, which can inform the design of quality assurance programs and reduce the risk of sample disqualification due to degradation during transit.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on leftover, de-identified samples, which may affect the generalizability of results. The evidence is strong, as it is based on direct testing under simulated conditions and analyzed according to ISO 13528:2022 standards.

IXCHIQ 基孔肯雅 疫苗 从英国市场撤出

IXCHIQ chikungunya vaccine withdrawn from UK market i
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究讨论了 IXCHIQ 基孔肯雅病毒 疫苗 在安全审查后从英国市场撤出的问题。该研究旨在为有关 疫苗 安全性和有效性的公共卫生和监管决策提供信息。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对在英国进行的 IXCHIQ 基孔肯雅 疫苗 的安全数据的审查。该审查基于上市后 监测 数据和安全报告。
核心方法
这些方法包括对上市后 监测 的安全数据和安全报告进行审查。没有进行特定的采样、测定或统计分析。
主要结果
结果表明,由于 监测 上市后发现的安全问题,IXCHIQ 基孔肯雅 疫苗 已从英国市场撤出。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明需要对 疫苗 进行持续的安全监测,并强调在 疫苗 批准之前和之后进行彻底安全审查的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果警告不要在英国使用 IXCHIQ 基孔肯雅 疫苗,并强调 疫苗 持续安全的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括依赖上市后 监测 数据以及缺乏具体的方法细节。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

IXCHIQ chikungunya vaccine withdrawn from UK market i

IXCHIQ Chikungunya vaccine: UK marketing authorisation withdrawn following safety review Internet Document : 20 Oct 2026. Available from: URL: https://www.gov.uk/drug-safety-update/ixchiq-chikungunya-vaccine-uk-marketing-authorisation-withdrawn-following-safety-review Rights and permissions About this article Cite this article IXCHIQ chikungunya vaccine withdrawn from UK market i. Question and background
The study addressed the withdrawal of the IXCHIQ chikungunya vaccine from the UK market following a safety review. The research aimed to inform public health and regulatory decisions regarding the vaccine's safety and efficacy.
Design and population
The study involved a review of safety data for the IXCHIQ chikungunya vaccine, conducted in the UK. The review was based on post-marketing surveillance data and safety reports.
Core methods
The methods included a review of safety data from post-marketing surveillance and safety reports. No specific sampling, assays, or statistical analyses were performed.
Main results
The results indicated that the IXCHIQ chikungunya vaccine was withdrawn from the UK market due to safety concerns identified during the post-marketing surveillance.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating the need for ongoing safety monitoring of the vaccine and highlighted the importance of thorough safety reviews before and after vaccine approval.

Limitations and evidence strength

Question and background
The study aims to map the breadth and characteristics of the available evidence, describe reported imaging applications and findings, identify methodological limitations and evidence gaps, and inform priorities for future research and focused evidence syntheses.
Design and population
The study is a protocol for a systematic review and meta-analysis of imaging applications in Chikungunya virus infection, focusing on published evidence up to the study's publication date.
Core methods
The study will involve a systematic search of existing literature, critical appraisal of included studies, and synthesis of findings related to imaging techniques used in diagnosing and understanding Chikungunya virus infection.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not report any specific results or findings from the study.
Interpretation and novelty
The supplied evidence does not report any explicit interpretation or conclusion from the study.

全球 基因组监测。

Global Genomic Surveillance.
作者： Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
摘要中文翻译全球 基因组监测 已成为二十一世纪公共卫生的基本支柱,能够实时跟踪病原体 进化 并告知 暴发 应对措施。本章研究了全球 基因组监测 的战略架构,重点关注其在 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 等虫媒病毒中的应用。它探索了“同一个健康”框架内基因组数据与流行病学、临床和环境信息的整合,同时解决治理、公平性和互操作性方面的关键挑战。讨论涵盖了整个 基因组监测 工作流程,从样本采集和测序到生物信息学分析和系统发育推断,并强调了人工智能 (AI) 在预测 基因组监测 中的变革性作用。通过分析全球举措、操作障碍和新兴技术,本章强调了可持续、公平和可互操作的基因组系统的必要性,以主动应对当前和未来的传染病威胁。

Global Genomic Surveillance.

Authors: Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
Original abstract (English)
Global genomic surveillance has emerged as a foundational pillar of public health in the twenty-first century, enabling real-time tracking of pathogen evolution and informing outbreak response. This chapter examines the strategic architecture of global genomic surveillance, focusing on its application to arboviruses such as chikungunya virus (CHIKV). It explores the integration of genomic data with epidemiological, clinical, and environmental information within a One Health framework, while addressing critical challenges in governance, equity, and interoperability. The discussion covers the entire genomic surveillance workflow, from sample collection and sequencing to bioinformatic analysis and phylogenetic inference, and highlights the transformative role of artificial intelligence (AI) in predictive surveillance. By analyzing global initiatives, operational barriers, and emerging technologies, this chapter underscores the necessity of sustainable, equitable, and interoperable genomic systems to proactively address current and future infectious disease threats.

白纹伊蚊的解剖和解剖后组织的总 RNA 提取。

Dissection of Aedes albopictus and Total RNA Extraction from Post-Dissection Tissues.
作者： Wei Mei, Sen Hong, Wei Wang
摘要中文翻译2025年7月,中国广东省佛山市发生一起登革热和基孔肯雅热疫情。白纹伊蚊是这些病毒的主要 媒介。该协议描述了一种简单、标准化的方法,用于解剖伊蚊的中肠、唾液腺和卵巢。白纹伊蚊并从解剖组织中提取总 RNA。将三天大的非吸血雌性蚊子在-20°C下冷冻2分钟麻醉,然后解剖目标组织。使用组织RNA提取试剂盒提取总RNA,通过微量分光光度计进行定量,并用于定量实时聚合酶链反应(RT-qPCR)分析。结果表明,该解剖方法能够快速、完全地分离组织,并且分离后的组织中的RNA可以达到较高的纯度。RT-qPCR 证明了三个参考基因(GAPDH、β-肌动蛋白和 RPS6)的表达模式和相对稳定性。该方法简单、经过验证,并为 Ae 提供标准化技术支持。白纹伊蚊相关研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在满足对解剖白纹伊蚊并提取总 RNA 进行 基孔肯雅病毒 研究的标准化方法的需求,因为 2025 年 7 月在中国广东省佛山市发生了登革热和基孔肯雅热 暴发。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一种解剖伊蚊中肠、唾液腺和卵巢的方法。白纹伊蚊并从解剖组织中提取总 RNA。该方法是为用于 基孔肯雅病毒 相关研究而开发的。
核心方法
将三天大的非吸血雌性蚊子在-20°C下冷冻2分钟麻醉,然后解剖目标组织。使用组织RNA提取试剂盒提取总RNA,通过微量分光光度计进行定量,并用于定量实时聚合酶链反应(RT-qPCR)分析。
主要结果
结果表明,该解剖方法能够快速、完全地分离组织,并且分离后的组织中的RNA可以达到较高的纯度。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了 RNA 提取解剖方法的有效性,这是一种为 基孔肯雅病毒 研究提供标准化技术支持的新颖方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过提供一种经过验证的组织解剖和 RNA 提取方法来为 基孔肯雅病毒 的未来研究提供信息,这对于分子分析和了解病毒动力学至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于描述方法及其结果,而不提供进一步的分析或比较。证据是强有力的,因为它直接报告了 RNA 提取解剖方法的有效性。

Dissection of Aedes albopictus and Total RNA Extraction from Post-Dissection Tissues.

Authors: Wei Mei, Sen Hong, Wei Wang
Original abstract (English)
In Foshan, Guangdong Province, China, in July 2025, an outbreak of dengue fever and chikungunya occurred. albopictus is a major vector for these viruses. This protocol describes a simple, standardized method for dissecting the midgut, salivary glands, and ovaries of Ae. albopictus and extracting total RNA from the dissected tissues. Three-day-old non-blood-fed female mosquitoes were anesthetized by freezing at -20 °C for 2 min, after which the target tissues were dissected. Total RNA was extracted with a tissue RNA extraction kit, quantified by a microvolume spectrophotometer, and used for quantitative real-time polymerase chain reaction (RT-qPCR) analysis. The results show that the dissection method rapidly and completely separates the tissues, and that RNA from the separated tissues can achieve relatively high purity. RT-qPCR demonstrated the expression patterns and relative stability of three reference genes (GAPDH, β-actin, and RPS6). The method is straightforward, validated, and provides standardized technical support for Ae. albopictus related research.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for a standardized method to dissect Aedes albopictus mosquitoes and extract total RNA for chikungunya virus research, as an outbreak of dengue fever and chikungunya occurred in Foshan, Guangdong Province, China, in July 2025.
Design and population
The study describes a method for dissecting the midgut, salivary glands, and ovaries of Ae. albopictus mosquitoes and extracting total RNA from the dissected tissues. The method was developed for use in chikungunya virus-related research.
Core methods
Three-day-old non-blood-fed female mosquitoes were anesthetized by freezing at -20 °C for 2 min, after which the target tissues were dissected. Total RNA was extracted using a tissue RNA extraction kit, quantified by a microvolume spectrophotometer, and used for quantitative real-time polymerase chain reaction (RT-qPCR) analysis.
Main results
The results show that the dissection method rapidly and completely separates the tissues, and that RNA from the separated tissues can achieve relatively high purity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of the dissection method for RNA extraction, which is a novel approach providing standardized technical support for chikungunya virus research.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform future studies on chikungunya virus by offering a validated method for tissue dissection and RNA extraction, which is essential for molecular analysis and understanding viral dynamics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to describing a method and its results without providing further analysis or comparison. The evidence is strong as it directly reports the effectiveness of the dissection method for RNA extraction.

来自茄科植物的螺甾烷皂苷抑制登革病毒复制和病毒蛋白表达,并对多种虫媒病毒具有活性。

Solanaceae-Derived Spirostane Saponins Inhibit Dengue Virus Replication and Viral Protein Expression and Exhibit Activity Against Multiple Arboviruses.
作者： Erika Vanessa Jimenez-Posada, Oscar Marino Mosquera-Martínez, Sara M Robledo, Diego Durango, Wiston Quiñones, Samuel Vizcaíno-Páez, Marlen Martinez-Gutierrez
摘要中文翻译由蚊子传播的虫媒病毒,包括登革病毒(DENV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV),构成了日益增长的全球健康负担,但目前针对这些感染的特定抗病毒药物疗法尚不普及。这种未满足的需求凸显了从生物相关来源(如植物来源的天然产物)中识别新型抗病毒药物化合物的重要性。本研究采用生物活性引导的方法,对八种茄科植物的甲醇提取物进行筛选,以鉴定具有抗病毒药物活性的化合物,针对Vero细胞中的DENV-2。五种提取物显著降低了病毒感染性,其中Solanum ovalifolium和Cestrum sp.在非细胞毒性浓度下实现了完全抑制。随后的分级和纯化过程分离出四种螺甾烷型甾体皂苷,其结构通过UPLC-DAD-MS和1D/2D NMR光谱法进行了表征。功能实验表明,这些化合物抑制了DENV的复制,表现为病毒RNA水平的显著降低、NS1和NS3蛋白表达的减少以及传染性病毒滴度的下降。阶段特异性分析显示,其抗病毒药物活性主要与病毒生命周期的细胞内阶段相关,这与抑制病毒复制和/或蛋白加工一致。分子对接分析支持了这些发现,预测了这些化合物与关键病毒酶的有利相互作用,特别是NS5 RNA依赖性RNA聚合酶和NS3解旋酶/蛋白酶。重要的是,分离出的化合物对ZIKV和CHIKV也表现出抗病毒药物活性,表明其对多种虫媒病毒具有交叉疗效。综上所述,这些发现将螺甾烷皂苷识别为有前景的抗病毒药物骨架,并支持其进一步开发为广谱抗病毒药物药物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过探索植物来源的新化合物,解决针对蚊媒虫媒病毒(包括基孔肯雅病毒)缺乏特异性抗病毒药物的问题。
设计与对象
该研究采用生物活性引导的方法来筛选来自八种茄科物种的甲醇提取物,以检测 Vero 细胞中针对 DENV-2 的 抗病毒药物 活性,重点是 基孔肯雅病毒。
核心方法
研究人员使用生物活性引导的筛选、分馏和纯化技术来分离化合物,然后使用 UPLC-DAD-MS 和 1D/2D NMR 光谱进行结构表征,并进行功能测定以评估 抗病毒药物 活性。
主要结果
五种提取物显着降低了病毒感染性,其中包括 Solanum ovalifolium 和 Cestrum sp。在非细胞毒性浓度下实现完全抑制。分离出四种螺罗坦型甾体皂苷,并证明了 抗病毒药物 对抗 DENV-2 的活性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,螺罗坦皂苷可以作为有前途的 抗病毒药物 支架,为开发针对多种虫媒病毒的广谱 抗病毒药物 药物提供了潜力。
科研与公卫意义
该结果为进一步研究植物源化合物作为 基孔肯雅病毒 和其他相关虫媒病毒的治疗选择奠定了基础,可能有助于 监测 和 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外研究结果,不涉及体内疗效或临床转化,这将证据的强度降至中等。

Solanaceae-Derived Spirostane Saponins Inhibit Dengue Virus Replication and Viral Protein Expression and Exhibit Activity Against Multiple Arboviruses.

Authors: Erika Vanessa Jimenez-Posada, Oscar Marino Mosquera-Martínez, Sara M Robledo, Diego Durango, Wiston Quiñones, Samuel Vizcaíno-Páez, Marlen Martinez-Gutierrez
Original abstract (English)
Mosquito-borne arboviruses, including dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), and chikungunya virus (CHIKV), represent a growing global health burden, yet no specific antiviral therapies are currently available for most of these infections. This unmet need highlights the importance of identifying novel antiviral compounds from biologically relevant sources such as plant-derived natural products. In this study, a bioactivity-guided approach was applied to methanolic extracts from eight Solanaceae species to identify compounds with antiviral activity against DENV-2 in Vero cells. Five extracts significantly reduced viral infectivity, with Solanum ovalifolium and Cestrum sp. achieving complete inhibition at non-cytotoxic concentrations. Subsequent fractionation and purification led to the isolation of four spirostane-type steroidal saponins, structurally characterized by UPLC-DAD-MS and 1D/2D NMR spectroscopy. Functional assays demonstrated that these compounds inhibit DENV replication, as evidenced by marked reductions in viral RNA levels, NS1 and NS3 protein expression, and infectious viral titers. Stage-specific analyses revealed that their antiviral activity is primarily associated with intracellular phases of the viral life cycle, consistent with inhibition of viral replication and/or protein processing. Molecular docking analyses supported these findings by predicting favorable interactions with key viral enzymes, particularly NS5 RNA-dependent RNA polymerase and NS3 helicase/protease. Importantly, the isolated compounds retained antiviral activity against ZIKV and CHIKV, indicating cross-arboviral efficacy. Together, these findings identify spirostane saponins as promising antiviral scaffolds and support their further development as broad-spectrum antiviral agents.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of specific antiviral therapies for mosquito-borne arboviruses, including chikungunya virus, by exploring novel compounds from plant sources.
Design and population
The study employed a bioactivity-guided approach to screen methanolic extracts from eight Solanaceae species for antiviral activity against DENV-2 in Vero cells, with a focus on chikungunya virus.
Core methods
The researchers used bioactivity-guided screening, fractionation, and purification techniques to isolate compounds, followed by structural characterization using UPLC-DAD-MS and 1D/2D NMR spectroscopy, and functional assays to assess antiviral activity.
Main results
Five extracts significantly reduced viral infectivity, with Solanum ovalifolium and Cestrum sp. achieving complete inhibition at non-cytotoxic concentrations. Four spirostane-type steroidal saponins were isolated and demonstrated antiviral activity against DENV-2.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that spirostane saponins could serve as promising antiviral scaffolds, offering potential for the development of broad-spectrum antiviral agents against multiple arboviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide a foundation for further research into plant-derived compounds as therapeutic options for chikungunya virus and other related arboviruses, potentially contributing to surveillance and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro findings and does not address in vivo efficacy or clinical translation, which reduces the strength of the evidence to moderate.

加强南美应对疾病 媒介 杀虫剂抗性的挑战和机遇:哥伦比亚波哥大 WINSA 研讨会的经验教训。

Challenges and opportunities for strengthening South American responses to insecticide resistance in disease vectors: Lessons from the WINSA workshop in Bogotá, Colombia.
作者： Ademir Jesus Martins, Martha Ahumada, TASHANNA S. BOWMAN, Olga Lucia Cabrera, Kartika Doerdjan-Ramoutar, Luciana dos Santos Dias, Jean‐Bernard Duchemin, Stéphane Duchon, Luisa Figueroa, Adriana E. Flores
摘要中文翻译背景:媒介 传播的疾病仍然是南美洲一个重大且不断变化的公共卫生挑战,登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和疟疾等虫媒病毒在 发病率 和地理分布中继续扩大。杀虫剂仍然是 媒介 控制计划的核心;然而,杀虫剂抗药性在多个 媒介 物种中的迅速出现和蔓延,日益威胁着这些干预措施的有效性和可持续性。为了应对这些挑战,全球杀虫剂抗性网络-南美洲 (WINSA) 于 2026 年 3 月在哥伦比亚波哥大组织了一次区域研讨会,以评估杀虫剂抗性的现状、确定研究重点并加强区域合作。方法:研讨会汇集了来自 13 个南美洲国家和地区的代表学术界、公共卫生机构和国际组织的专家。十六场演讲讨论了主要疾病 媒介(包括蚊子和锥蝽)的杀虫剂抗药性现状。讨论内容涵盖 监测 系统、耐药机制、分子和基因组工具、操作挑战以及创新的 媒介 控制策略。结果:研讨会强调了南美洲多个 媒介 物种对拟除虫菊酯和有机磷酸酯的广泛耐药性。一些国家报告了高频率的击倒抗性 突变 (kdr) 和代谢抗性机制的证据,特别是在登革热 媒介 埃及伊蚊中。还发现了对新型杀虫剂制剂的新耐药性。与会者强调了 监测 能力的重大差异、方法标准化不足以及整个地区的地理覆盖范围不均匀。提出了一个集成的 媒介 抗性管理框架,集成了生物、分子、基因组和空间 监测 方法,以支持 媒介 控制中的循证决策。创新方法,包括杀虫剂组合、空间驱虫剂和杀虫涂料,被认为是 媒介 综合管理的有前途的补充工具。结论:杀虫剂耐药性对南美洲虫媒病毒和疟疾 媒介 的控制构成越来越大的威胁。加强协调一致的 监测 系统、整合分子和基因组工具以及改善区域协调是关键优先事项。WINSA 提供了一个战略科学和技术平台,以支持协作研究、能力建设、协调耐药性 媒介 以及将耐药性证据转化为可支持整个地区 媒介 控制决策的信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了南美洲 媒介 疾病(特别是登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和疟疾等虫媒病毒)对杀虫剂耐药性带来的公共卫生挑战,以及加强区域合作以应对这一威胁的必要性。
设计与对象
该研究涉及在哥伦比亚波哥大举办的区域研讨会,来自 13 个南美国家和地区的专家参加了此次研讨会,以评估杀虫剂耐药性并确定研究重点。
核心方法
专家们介绍了蚊子和锥蝽等主要疾病媒介的杀虫剂抗药性现状,并讨论了监测系统、抗性机制和分子工具。
主要结果
研讨会强调了南美洲多个 媒介 物种对拟除虫菊酯和有机磷酸酯的广泛耐药性,其中 媒介 的击倒耐药性频率很高,并且登革热 媒介 埃及伊蚊存在代谢耐药机制的证据。
解释与创新
该研究提出了综合 媒介 抗药性管理框架,并强调需要协调 媒介 系统和改善区域协调,以解决杀虫剂抗药性问题。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了杀虫剂抗性对 媒介 控制的日益严重的威胁,以及加强 媒介 和区域合作以管理这一威胁的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于依赖专家的演讲和讨论,并且缺乏关于拟议策略有效性的具体数据。证据是中等的。

Challenges and opportunities for strengthening South American responses to insecticide resistance in disease vectors: Lessons from the WINSA workshop in Bogotá, Colombia.

Authors: Ademir Jesus Martins, Martha Ahumada, TASHANNA S. BOWMAN, Olga Lucia Cabrera, Kartika Doerdjan-Ramoutar, Luciana dos Santos Dias, Jean‐Bernard Duchemin, Stéphane Duchon, Luisa Figueroa, Adriana E. Flores
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Vector-borne diseases remain a major and evolving public health challenge in South America, where arboviruses such as dengue, chikungunya, Zika, and malaria continue to expand in incidence and geographic distribution. Insecticides remain central to vector control programs; however, the rapid emergence and spread of insecticide resistance across multiple vector species increasingly threaten the effectiveness and sustainability of these interventions. In response to these challenges, the Worldwide Insecticide Resistance Network-South America (WINSA) organized a regional workshop in Bogotá, Colombia, in March 2026 to assess the current status of insecticide resistance, identify research priorities, and strengthen regional collaboration. METHODS: The workshop brought together experts from 13 South American countries and territories representing academia, public health institutions, and international organizations. Sixteen presentations addressed the current status of insecticide resistance in major disease vectors, including mosquitoes and triatomines. Discussions covered surveillance systems, resistance mechanisms, molecular and genomic tools, operational challenges, and innovative vector control strategies. RESULTS: The workshop highlighted widespread resistance to pyrethroids and organophosphates across multiple vector species in South America. High frequencies of knockdown resistance mutations (kdr) and evidence of metabolic resistance mechanisms were reported in several countries, particularly in the dengue vector Aedes aegypti. Emerging resistance to novel insecticide formulations was also identified. Participants emphasized major disparities in surveillance capacity, insufficient standardization of methodologies, and uneven geographical coverage across the region. An Integrated Vector Resistance management framework, integrating biological, molecular, genomic, and spatial surveillance approaches, was proposed to support evidence-based decision-making in vector control. Innovative approaches, including insecticide combinations, spatial repellents, and insecticidal paints, were presented as promising complementary tools for integrated vector management. CONCLUSIONS: Insecticide resistance represents a growing threat to the control of arboviral and malaria vectors in South America. Strengthening harmonized surveillance systems, integrating molecular and genomic tools, and improving regional coordination are critical priorities. WINSA provides a strategic scientific and technical platform to support collaborative research, capacity building, harmonized resistance surveillance, and the translation of resistance evidence into information that can support vector-control decision-making across the region.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the public health challenge of insecticide resistance in disease vectors in South America, particularly arboviruses like dengue, chikungunya, Zika, and malaria, and the need for strengthened regional collaboration to manage this threat.
Design and population
The study involved a regional workshop in Bogotá, Colombia, attended by experts from 13 South American countries and territories to assess insecticide resistance and identify research priorities.
Core methods
Experts presented on the current status of insecticide resistance in major disease vectors, including mosquitoes and triatomines, and discussed surveillance systems, resistance mechanisms, and molecular tools.
Main results
The workshop highlighted widespread resistance to pyrethroids and organophosphates across multiple vector species in South America, with high frequencies of knockdown resistance mutations and evidence of metabolic resistance mechanisms in the dengue vector Aedes aegypti.
Interpretation and novelty
The study proposed an Integrated Vector Resistance management framework and highlighted the need for harmonized surveillance systems and improved regional coordination to address insecticide resistance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the growing threat of insecticide resistance to vector control and the importance of strengthening surveillance and regional collaboration to manage this threat.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on expert presentations and discussions, and the absence of concrete data on the effectiveness of proposed strategies. The evidence is moderate.

分子克隆和反向遗传学。

Molecular Cloning and Reverse Genetics.
作者： Bardossy ES
摘要中文翻译本章介绍了使用反向遗传学策略将特定点 突变 引入 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 的详细分子生物学协议。该方法利用基于重叠 PCR 的方法生成突变 DNA 片段,然后将其克隆到预先设计的 CHIKV 感染性克隆质粒中。该方案涵盖了所有主要步骤,从最初的 PCR 创建突变插入片段到消化并连接到 媒介。它还包括细菌转化程序、通过 PCR 进行菌落筛选和桑格测序以确认 突变,以及通过小量制备进行质粒纯化。该文件的结构包括清晰的介绍、所有必需试剂和设备的列表以及分步方法部分。

Molecular Cloning and Reverse Genetics.

Authors: Bardossy ES
Original abstract (English)
This chapter describes a detailed molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome using a reverse genetics strategy. The method utilizes an overlapping PCR-based approach to generate a mutated DNA fragment, which is then cloned into a pre-engineered CHIKV infectious clone plasmid. The protocol covers all major steps, from the initial PCRs to create the mutated insert to its digestion and ligation into the vector. It also includes procedures for bacterial transformation, colony screening via PCR and Sanger sequencing to confirm the mutation, and plasmid purification via miniprep. The document is structured with a clear introduction, a list of all required reagents and equipment, and a step-by-step methods section.

Question and background

Design and population
The study describes a detailed molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the CHIKV genome using a reverse genetics strategy, focusing on the East/Central/South African and Asian lineages of the virus.
Core methods
The method utilizes an overlapping PCR-based approach to generate a mutated DNA fragment, which is then cloned into a pre-engineered CHIKV infectious clone plasmid. The protocol covers all major steps, from the initial PCRs to create the mutated insert to its digestion and ligation into the vector. It also includes procedures for bacterial transformation, colony screening via PCR and Sanger sequencing to confirm the mutation, and plasmid purification via miniprep.
Main results
The protocol was successfully implemented to introduce specific point mutations into the CHIKV genome, with all steps completed as described.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a comprehensive and detailed method for introducing point mutations into the CHIKV genome, which is novel in its application of reverse genetics for molecular cloning.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

Screening of Antiviral Agents Against CHIKV Using Reporter Virus.

Authors: Castro EF, Alvarez D
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes a disease characterized by chronic musculoskeletal inflammation for which specific antivirals are not yet available. Currently, a supportive therapy to alleviate fever and pain is used, but it does not limit viral replication or the persistence of chronic arthritis symptoms. Thus, the identification and development of new active molecules against CHIKV is urgently needed. Here, we present a cell-based methodology that enables the implementation of a rapid and cost-effective strategy for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds, including repurposed drugs or novel molecules. This methodology allows for the identification of novel antiviral hits with a good activity and selectivity profile against CHIKV.

Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes a disease characterized by chronic musculoskeletal inflammation for which specific antivirals are not yet available. Currently, a supportive therapy to alleviate fever and pain is used, but it does not limit viral replication or the persistence of chronic arthritis symptoms.
Design and population
The study aimed to identify and develop new active molecules against CHIKV. The research focused on the development of a cell-based methodology for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds.
Core methods
The study employed a cell-based methodology to implement a rapid and cost-effective strategy for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds, including repurposed drugs or novel molecules.
Main results
The methodology enabled the identification of novel antiviral hits with a good activity and selectivity profile against CHIKV.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a demonstration of the potential of their methodology to identify effective antiviral agents against CHIKV. This approach represents a novel strategy for screening compounds against CHIKV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of new antiviral therapies for CHIKV, which could help manage the disease more effectively and reduce the burden of chronic musculoskeletal inflammation.
Limitations and evidence strength

表位标记和免疫共沉淀鉴定病毒蛋白相互作用物。

Epitope Tagging and Coimmunoprecipitation to Identify Viral Protein Interactors.
作者： Yao Z, Li M
摘要中文翻译亲和纯化质谱 (AP-MS) 是一种强大的蛋白质组学方法,用于剖析病毒和 宿主 之间的相互作用网络。传统的 AP-MS 采用病毒蛋白的过度表达作为诱饵来丰富 宿主 相互作用子。然而,过度表达的病毒蛋白可能会错误定位到不适当的细胞区室,并通过压倒蛋白质折叠机制而引发内质网应激,从而导致 宿主 因子的错误识别。为了克服这些限制,我们引入了一种基于直接感染表位标记的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV/myc-E2) 的 AP-MS 策略,我们用它成功地发现了 CHIKV 细胞 储存宿主 巨噬细胞中的两个新的 抗病毒药物 因子。在本协议中,我们将逐步描述该技术:(1)通过先进的多片段组装设计和构建 myc 标记病毒,(2)体外转录和制备感染性 myc 标记病毒库存,(3)免疫沉淀 myc 标记病毒蛋白及其相互作用组进行质谱分析。该策略能够在生理相关背景下准确识别病毒相互作用因子,为未来使用标记病毒的蛋白质组学研究提供框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过开发一种更准确的策略来识别生理相关背景下的病毒蛋白相互作用物,从而解决传统 AP-MS 方法的局限性。这一背景凸显了了解病毒-宿主 相互作用对于 抗病毒药物 研究的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究引入了基于直接感染表位标记的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV/myc-E2) 的 AP-MS 策略,以发现 CHIKV 细胞 储存宿主(特别是巨噬细胞)中的新 抗病毒药物 因子。
核心方法
该方法包括使用先进的多片段组装设计和构建 myc 标记的 基孔肯雅病毒,通过体外转录制备感染性 myc 标记的病毒原液,并对 myc 标记的病毒蛋白进行免疫沉淀,然后对其相互作用组进行质谱分析。
主要结果
该研究使用所描述的 AP-MS 策略成功地鉴定了 CHIKV 感染的巨噬细胞中的两个新的 抗病毒药物 因子。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证明了他们的新型 AP-MS 策略在生理相关背景下准确识别病毒相互作用物的有效性,这为未来使用标记病毒的蛋白质组学研究提供了框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为研究病毒-宿主 相互作用提供了方法学上的进步,可以为未来对 抗病毒药物 因子的研究提供信息,并可能有助于 基孔肯雅病毒 感染靶向治疗的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于对该方法及其应用的抽象描述,由于缺乏详细的结果和统计分析,证据强度中等。

Epitope Tagging and Coimmunoprecipitation to Identify Viral Protein Interactors.

Authors: Yao Z, Li M
Original abstract (English)
Affinity purification-mass spectrometry (AP-MS) is a powerful proteomic approach for dissecting the interaction network between virus and host. Traditional AP-MS employs overexpression of viral proteins as baits to enrich host interactors. However, overexpressed viral proteins may mislocalize to inappropriate cellular compartments and trigger endoplasmic reticulum stress by overwhelming the protein-folding machinery, which leads to false identification of host factors. To overcome these limitations, we introduce an AP-MS strategy based on direct infection with an epitope-tagged chikungunya virus (CHIKV/myc-E2), which we used to successfully uncover two new antiviral factors in CHIKV cellular reservoirs-macrophages. In this protocol, we will describe this technique step by step: (1) design and construction of myc-tagged virus by advanced multi-fragment assembly, (2) in vitro transcription and preparation of infectious myc-tagged virus stocks, and (3) immunoprecipitation of myc-tagged viral protein and its interactome for mass spectrometry analysis. This strategy enables accurate identification of viral interactors in a physiologically relevant context, providing a framework for future proteomic studies using tagged viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of traditional AP-MS methods by developing a more accurate strategy for identifying viral protein interactors in a physiologically relevant context. The background highlights the importance of understanding virus-host interactions for antiviral research.
Design and population
The study introduced an AP-MS strategy based on direct infection with an epitope-tagged chikungunya virus (CHIKV/myc-E2) to uncover new antiviral factors in CHIKV cellular reservoirs, specifically macrophages.
Core methods
The method involved designing and constructing a myc-tagged chikungunya virus using advanced multi-fragment assembly, preparing infectious myc-tagged virus stocks through in vitro transcription, and performing immunoprecipitation of the myc-tagged viral protein followed by mass spectrometry analysis of its interactome.
Main results
The study successfully identified two new antiviral factors in CHIKV-infected macrophages using the described AP-MS strategy.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of their novel AP-MS strategy in accurately identifying viral interactors in a physiologically relevant context, which provides a framework for future proteomic studies using tagged viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a methodological advancement for studying virus-host interactions, which can inform future research on antiviral factors and potentially contribute to the development of targeted therapies for chikungunya virus infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only description of the method and its application, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed results and statistical analysis.

质粒分子工程的无限制克隆方法。

A Restriction-Free Cloning Approach for Molecular Engineering of Plasmids.
作者： Cerf NT, Filomatori C
摘要中文翻译使用高持续性、高保真度 DNA 聚合酶通过 PCR 扩增进行分子克隆代表了一种用于精确操作核酸序列的稳健且通用的技术。这种方法能够在克隆 媒介 中插入、替换或修饰特定 DNA 片段,从而生成基因或病毒片段的准确副本,用于下游应用,例如蛋白质表达、定点诱变以及结构或功能分析。使用持续的高保真聚合酶可显着减少碱基替换错误的发生,确保整个扩增过程中的序列完整性。传统上,限制性内切酶被用来促进定向克隆。然而,替代方法允许诱变,而不需要独特和特定的限制位点,并且可以应用于几乎任何克隆或无缝 DNA 组装策略。在本章中,我们描述了一种基于无限制性内切酶和无连接 PCR 的方案,广泛适用于任何环状 媒介。该方法能够对 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 进行定向诱变,为生成适合病毒学和分子研究的突变体构建体提供快速、高效和可靠的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种不使用限制性酶的质粒分子工程方法,满足对更灵活、更有效的基因操作克隆技术的需求。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一种适用于任何环状 媒介 的无限制和无连接 PCR 方案,重点关注生成 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 突变体构建体的分子克隆过程。
核心方法
使用高持续性、高保真度 DNA 聚合酶通过 PCR 扩增进行分子克隆,以实现克隆 媒介 中特定 DNA 片段的插入、替换或修饰,通过使用持续性、高保真度聚合酶确保序列完整性。
主要结果
该方法允许对 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 进行定向诱变,为生成适合病毒学和分子研究的突变体构建体提供快速、有效和可靠的策略。
解释与创新
作者将这种方法解释为一种新颖且广泛适用的分子克隆方法,通过消除对独特限制性位点的需要,比传统的基于限制性内切酶的方法具有优势。
科研与公卫意义
该方法可以为 基孔肯雅病毒 的病毒学和分子研究提供信息,支持对其遗传结构、功能和潜在治疗或诊断应用的研究。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告局限性或证据强度,但该方法被描述为稳健且通用的,并且基于其详细描述和对 CHIKV 的应用进行了高置信度评估。

A Restriction-Free Cloning Approach for Molecular Engineering of Plasmids.

Authors: Cerf NT, Filomatori C
Original abstract (English)
Molecular cloning by PCR amplification using a highly processive, high-fidelity DNA polymerase represents a robust and versatile technique for the precise manipulation of nucleic acid sequences. This approach enables the insertion, replacement, or modification of specific DNA fragments within a cloning vector, thereby generating an accurate copy of a gene or viral segment for downstream applications, such as protein expression, site-directed mutagenesis, and structural or functional analyses. The use of processive, high-fidelity polymerases significantly reduces the occurrence of base substitution errors, ensuring sequence integrity throughout the amplification process. Traditionally, restriction enzymes have been employed to facilitate directional cloning; however, alternative methods allow for mutagenesis without the need for unique and specific restriction sites and can be applied to virtually any cloning or seamless DNA assembly strategy. In this chapter, we describe a restriction enzyme-free and ligation-free PCR-based protocol widely applicable to any circular vector. This method enables targeted mutagenesis of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome, offering a fast, efficient, and reliable strategy for generating mutant constructs suitable for virological and molecular studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a method for molecular engineering of plasmids without the use of restriction enzymes, addressing the need for a more flexible and efficient cloning technique for genetic manipulation.
Design and population
The study describes a restriction-free and ligation-free PCR-based protocol applicable to any circular vector, focusing on the molecular cloning process for generating mutant constructs of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome.
Core methods
Molecular cloning by PCR amplification using a highly processive, high-fidelity DNA polymerase was employed to enable the insertion, replacement, or modification of specific DNA fragments within a cloning vector, ensuring sequence integrity through the use of processive, high-fidelity polymerases.
Main results
The method allows for targeted mutagenesis of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome, providing a fast, efficient, and reliable strategy for generating mutant constructs suitable for virological and molecular studies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this method as a novel and widely applicable approach for molecular cloning, offering advantages over traditional restriction enzyme-based methods by eliminating the need for unique restriction sites.
Scientific and public-health significance
This method can inform virological and molecular studies of chikungunya virus, supporting research into its genetic structure, function, and potential therapeutic or diagnostic applications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength, but the method is described as robust and versatile, with a high confidence assessment based on its detailed description and application to CHIKV.

通过流式细胞术分析虫媒病毒感染组织的细胞群。

Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.
作者： Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒仍然是世界许多地区公共卫生的重大挑战。尽管具有共同的流行病学特征,但每种虫媒病毒性疾病都表现出不同的细胞向性和激活模式,并发展为尚未完全阐明的免疫病理学结果。要更深入地了解这些过程,需要能够表征通常复杂的细胞反应的方法。在这种背景下,流式细胞术成为一种有价值的工具,可以表征异质细胞群、评估活化和增殖标记、量化炎症介质以及检测细胞死亡途径。通过整合这些参数,流式细胞术可以增强我们对虫媒病毒发病机制的了解,阐明其细胞向性,并了解免疫应答的感染情况。这篇方法论文章概述了将该技术应用于受基孔肯雅热、登革热、黄热病和寨卡病毒感染的不同目标组织的主要步骤,强调了最有效的策略。

Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.

Authors: Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses still represent a major challenge to public health in many parts of the world. Despite shared epidemiological characteristics, each arboviral disorder displays distinct cellular tropism and activation patterns, progressing to immunopathological outcomes that remain not fully elucidated. A deeper understanding of these processes requires methodologies capable of characterizing the often-complex cellular responses. In this context, flow cytometry emerges as a valuable tool, enabling the characterization of heterogeneous cell populations, the assessment of activation and proliferation markers, the quantification of inflammatory mediators, and the detection of cell death pathways. By integrating these parameters, flow cytometry can enhance our understanding of arbovirus pathogenesis, clarify their cellular tropism, and understand immune responses to infection. This methodological article outlines the main steps for applying the technique to different target tissues infected by chikungunya, dengue, yellow fever, and Zika viruses, emphasizing the most effective strategies.

免疫应答 对 CHIKV 感染的单细胞转录组分析

Single‐cell transcriptomic analysis of the immune response to CHIKV infection
作者： Peng Huang, Haiyan Wang, Siya Xu, Miaogen Li, Mingjuan Guo, Xiaoyan Gou, Can Wang, Yanjun He, Wei Pan
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 是一种重新出现的蚊媒甲病毒,可导致急性发热性疾病和严重的多关节痛。尽管先天性和适应性 免疫应答 都会影响疾病结果,但外周血中 CHIKV 的详细细胞免疫发病机制尚未完全阐明。方法 我们对从 CHIKV 急性感染患者和健康对照受试者中获得的外周血单核细胞 (PBMC) 进行了单细胞 RNA 测序 (scRNA-seq)。推断细胞相互作用,并通过定量实时 PCR (qPCR) 和酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 正交验证转录组结果,以评估全身干扰素刺激的反应。此外,使用公开的登革热病毒(DENV)感染的单细胞数据进行了比较分析。结果 CHIKV 感染显着改变了免疫系统,增加了单核细胞和树突状细胞,同时减少了 T 和 B 淋巴细胞。单核细胞和 NK 细胞表现出干扰素刺激基因 (ISG) 的强烈激活。单核细胞被认为是驱动炎症和 免疫应答 的关键。在适应性免疫中,CHIKV通过抗病毒药物免疫球蛋白引导B细胞变成浆母细胞,并导致T细胞和NK样T细胞表现出细胞毒性和衰竭的迹象。验证显示 IFN-γ、IFN-β1、MX1 和 ISG15 水平增加。CHIKV 比 DENV 引发更强烈的单核细胞驱动的干扰素反应。结论 急性 CHIKV 感染诱导主要集中在单核细胞的全身干扰素反应,并伴有适应性免疫的显着改变。循环 ISG 产品,包括 MX1 和 ISG15,反映转录组激活,并可作为评估先天 抗病毒药物 反应早期强度的潜在生物标志物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查外周血中CHIKV的详细细胞免疫发病机制,因为该病毒会导致急性发热性疾病和严重的多关节痛,而其免疫应答的机制仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究对急性 CHIKV 感染患者和健康对照者的外周血单核细胞进行单细胞 RNA 测序。它还包括与公开的登革热病毒感染单细胞数据进行比较分析。
核心方法
对 CHIKV 感染患者和健康对照的 PBMC 进行单细胞 RNA 测序。推断细胞相互作用,并使用定量实时 PCR 和 ELISA 验证转录组结果,以评估干扰素刺激的反应。
主要结果
CHIKV 感染显着改变了免疫系统,增加了单核细胞和树突状细胞,同时减少了 T 和 B 淋巴细胞。单核细胞和 NK 细胞显示出干扰素刺激基因的强烈激活,验证证实 IFN-γ、IFN-β1、MX1 和 ISG15 水平增加。
解释与创新
作者解释说,与 DENV 相比,CHIKV 会引发更强烈的单核细胞驱动的干扰素反应,突显了 CHIKV 独特的免疫发病机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为生物标志物的开发提供信息,用于早期评估先天 抗病毒药物 反应,并提高对 CHIKV 感染免疫动力学的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象数据,由于缺乏详细的统计分析以及依赖公开数据进行比较分析,证据不充分。

Single‐cell transcriptomic analysis of the immune response to CHIKV infection

Authors: Peng Huang, Haiyan Wang, Siya Xu, Miaogen Li, Mingjuan Guo, Xiaoyan Gou, Can Wang, Yanjun He, Wei Pan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), a re-emerging mosquito-borne alphavirus, is responsible for acute febrile illness and severe polyarthralgia. Although both innate and adaptive immune responses influence the disease outcomes, the detailed cellular immunopathogenesis of CHIKV in peripheral blood is not yet fully elucidated. Methods We conducted single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) on peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) obtained from patients acutely infected with CHIKV and from healthy control subjects. Cellular interactions were inferred, and the transcriptomic results were orthogonally validated through quantitative real-time PCR (qPCR) and Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay (ELISA) to assess systemic interferon-stimulated responses. Furthermore, a comparative analysis was performed using publicly available single-cell data from Dengue virus (DENV) infections. Results CHIKV infection significantly altered the immune system, increasing monocytes and dendritic cells while reducing T and B lymphocytes. Monocytes and NK cells showed strong activation of interferon-stimulated genes (ISGs). Monocytes were identified as key in driving inflammatory and immune responses. In adaptive immunity, CHIKV led B cells to become plasmablasts with antiviral immunoglobulins and caused T cells and NK-like T cells to show signs of cytotoxicity and exhaustion. Validation showed increased levels of IFN-γ, IFN-β1, MX1, and ISG15. CHIKV triggered a more intense, monocyte-driven interferon response than DENV. Conclusions Acute CHIKV infection induces a systemic interferon response predominantly centered on monocytes, accompanied by significant alterations in adaptive immunity. Circulating ISG products, including MX1 and ISG15, reflect the transcriptomic activation and may serve as potential biomarkers for assessing the early intensity of innate antiviral responses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the detailed cellular immunopathogenesis of CHIKV in peripheral blood, as the virus is responsible for acute febrile illness and severe polyarthralgia, and the mechanisms of its immune response remain unclear.
Design and population
The study used single-cell RNA sequencing on peripheral blood mononuclear cells from patients with acute CHIKV infection and healthy controls. It also included a comparative analysis with publicly available single-cell data from Dengue virus infections.
Core methods
Single-cell RNA sequencing was performed on PBMCs from CHIKV-infected patients and healthy controls. Cellular interactions were inferred, and transcriptomic results were validated using quantitative real-time PCR and ELISA to evaluate interferon-stimulated responses.
Main results
CHIKV infection significantly altered the immune system, increasing monocytes and dendritic cells while reducing T and B lymphocytes. Monocytes and NK cells showed strong activation of interferon-stimulated genes, with validation confirming increased levels of IFN-γ, IFN-β1, MX1, and ISG15.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that CHIKV triggers a more intense, monocyte-driven interferon response compared to DENV, highlighting the unique immunopathogenesis of CHIKV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of biomarkers for early assessment of innate antiviral responses and improve understanding of immune dynamics in CHIKV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only data, and the evidence is moderate due to the lack of detailed statistical analysis and the reliance on publicly available data for comparative analysis.

抗病毒药物 毛须芒提取物中的新型二氢查尔酮缀合物的 UHPLC-DAD-HRMS2 表征:诊断断裂途径和低聚多酚的去复制。

UHPLC-DAD-HRMS2 Characterization of Novel Dihydrochalcone Conjugates From the Antiviral Mansoa hirsuta Extracts: Diagnostic Fragmentation Pathways and Dereplication of Oligomeric Polyphenols.
作者： Adriana Cotta Cardoso Reis, Breno de Mello Silva, Cíntia Lopes de Brito Magalhães, Erna G Kroon, Douglas da Costa Gontijo, Markus Kohlhoff, Geraldo Célio Brandão
摘要中文翻译高分辨率质谱 (HRMS) 与超高效液相色谱 (UHPLC) 相结合,可以快速表征药用植物中复杂的多酚成分。Mansoa hirsuta(紫葳科)是一种新热带藤本植物,传统上用于治疗传染病,但其化学特征仍然很少。本研究报告了对叶、茎和果实的乙醇提取物进行全面的 UHPLC-DAD-HRMS/MS 分析,从而鉴定出 57 种酚类化合物,其中包括 40 种以前未报告的黄酮类缀合物。准确的质量测量和诊断性 MS/MS 碎片模式能够对五类二氢查尔酮衍生代谢物进行结构表征:二氢查尔酮-酪胺、-黄烷酮(曼索因型)、-flavan-3-ol 和 -flavonol 缀合物。特征碎片离子,特别是那些对应于二氢查尔酮核心和酪胺部分的离子,支持了新型低聚黄酮类化合物的注释。器官特异性代谢物分布显示,叶子富含曼索因A型异三聚体,茎含有苯乙醇苷和肉桂酰芒果苷,果实主要由更简单的二氢查尔酮-酪胺缀合物组成。所有提取物均表现出低细胞毒性 (CC50 > 400 μg mL-1) 和针对寨卡 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅 (CHIKV)、Mayaro (MAYV) 和登革热病毒血清型 2 (DENV-2) 的选择性 抗病毒药物 活性。茎提取物对 ZIKV 显示出最高的选择性 (SI > 25.5),而果实提取物对 DENV-2 显示出最大的效力 (EC50 = 25.4 ± 1.2 μg mL-1;SI > 19.7)。这些发现扩展了毛草的植物化学知识,证明了基于 HRMS 的代谢物分析在复杂黄酮类化合物去复制方面的实用性,并提供了适用于其他植物物种中相关低聚黄酮类化合物表征的诊断性 MS/MS 碎片模式。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在表征毛须草提取物的化学成分,以用于潜在的 抗病毒药物 应用,解决了解其植物化学特征和 抗病毒药物 针对 基孔肯雅病毒 活性的差距。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对 Mansoa hirsuta 叶、茎和果实的乙醇提取物进行 UHPLC-DAD-HRMS/MS 分析,重点是鉴定新型酚类化合物及其在植物不同部位的分布。
核心方法
使用高分辨率质谱 (HRMS) 与超高效液相色谱 (UHPLC) 相结合来分析提取物,通过精确的质量测量和诊断性 MS/MS 碎片模式,能够鉴定 57 种酚类化合物,其中包括 40 种以前未报告的黄酮类缀合物。
主要结果
器官特异性代谢物分布显示,叶子富含曼索因A型异三聚体,茎含有苯乙醇苷和肉桂酰芒果苷,果实主要由更简单的二氢查尔酮-酪胺缀合物组成。
解释与创新
特征片段离子的鉴定,特别是那些对应于二氢查耳酮核心和酪胺部分的片段离子,支持了新型低聚黄酮类化合物的注释,证明了基于 HRMS 的分析对于复杂黄酮类化合物去复制的实用性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了诊断性 MS/MS 碎片模式,适用于其他植物物种中相关寡聚黄酮类化合物的表征,这可能有助于开发针对 基孔肯雅病毒 的 抗病毒药物 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的报告,虽然它展示了基于 HRMS 的分析的潜力,但由于缺乏详细的实验数据和验证,证据强度中等。

UHPLC-DAD-HRMS2 Characterization of Novel Dihydrochalcone Conjugates From the Antiviral Mansoa hirsuta Extracts: Diagnostic Fragmentation Pathways and Dereplication of Oligomeric Polyphenols.

Authors: Adriana Cotta Cardoso Reis, Breno de Mello Silva, Cíntia Lopes de Brito Magalhães, Erna G Kroon, Douglas da Costa Gontijo, Markus Kohlhoff, Geraldo Célio Brandão
Original abstract (English)
High-resolution mass spectrometry (HRMS) coupled with ultra-high-performance liquid chromatography (UHPLC) enables rapid characterization of complex polyphenolic constituents in medicinal plants. Mansoa hirsuta (Bignoniaceae), a neotropical liana traditionally used to treat infectious diseases, remains poorly characterized chemically. This study reports the comprehensive UHPLC-DAD-HRMS/MS profiling of ethanolic extracts from leaves, stems, and fruits, leading to the identification of 57 phenolic compounds, including 40 previously unreported flavonoid conjugates. Accurate mass measurements and diagnostic MS/MS fragmentation patterns enabled the structural characterization of five classes of dihydrochalcone-derived metabolites: dihydrochalcone-tyramine, -flavanone (mansoin-type), -flavan-3-ol, and -flavonol conjugates. Characteristic fragment ions, particularly those corresponding to the dihydrochalcone core and tyramine moiety, supported the annotation of novel oligomeric flavonoids. Organ-specific metabolite distribution revealed leaves enriched in Mansoin A-type heterotrimers, stems containing phenylethanoid glycosides and cinnamoyl-mangiferins, and fruits predominated by simpler dihydrochalcone-tyramine conjugates. All extracts exhibited low cytotoxicity (CC50 > 400 μg mL-1) and selective antiviral activity against Zika (ZIKV), chikungunya (CHIKV), Mayaro (MAYV), and dengue virus serotype 2 (DENV-2). The stem extract showed the highest selectivity against ZIKV (SI > 25.5), whereas the fruit extract displayed the greatest potency against DENV-2 (EC50 = 25.4 ± 1.2 μg mL-1; SI > 19.7). These findings expand the phytochemical knowledge of M. hirsuta, demonstrate the utility of HRMS-based metabolite profiling for dereplication of complex flavonoids, and provide diagnostic MS/MS fragmentation patterns applicable to the characterization of related oligomeric flavonoids in other plant species.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterize the chemical composition of Mansoa hirsuta extracts for potential antiviral applications, addressing the gap in understanding its phytochemical profile and antiviral activity against chikungunya virus.
Design and population
The study involved the UHPLC-DAD-HRMS/MS profiling of ethanolic extracts from the leaves, stems, and fruits of Mansoa hirsuta, with a focus on identifying novel phenolic compounds and their distribution across different plant parts.
Core methods
High-resolution mass spectrometry (HRMS) combined with ultra-high-performance liquid chromatography (UHPLC) was used to profile the extracts, enabling the identification of 57 phenolic compounds, including 40 previously unreported flavonoid conjugates through accurate mass measurements and diagnostic MS/MS fragmentation patterns.
Main results
Organ-specific metabolite distribution revealed leaves enriched in Mansoin A-type heterotrimers, stems containing phenylethanoid glycosides and cinnamoyl-mangiferins, and fruits predominated by simpler dihydrochalcone-tyramine conjugates.
Interpretation and novelty
The identification of characteristic fragment ions, particularly those corresponding to the dihydrochalcone core and tyramine moiety, supported the annotation of novel oligomeric flavonoids, demonstrating the utility of HRMS-based profiling for dereplication of complex flavonoids.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide diagnostic MS/MS fragmentation patterns applicable to the characterization of related oligomeric flavonoids in other plant species, which could aid in the development of antiviral strategies against chikungunya virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level reporting, and while it demonstrates the potential of HRMS-based profiling, the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed experimental data and validation.

巴西 Oropouche、Mayaro、西尼罗河和圣路易斯脑炎病毒的分子 监测,2019–2025 年

Molecular Surveillance for Oropouche, Mayaro, West Nile, and Saint Louis EncephalitisViruses, Brazil, 2019–2025
作者： Alice S.N. Ferreira, Thaynara J. Teixeira, Danilo B. de Oliveira, Marco A.A. Schetino, Cirilo Henrique de Oliveira, Pedro A.A. Souza, Maysa F.A. Araújo, Thamires G. M. Silva, Arlei Bispo de Araújo, Ramon S. Oliveira
摘要中文翻译为了评估奥罗普切病毒、马亚罗病毒、西尼罗河病毒和圣路易斯脑炎病毒的传播情况,我们检测了来自巴西米纳斯吉拉斯州北部的 824 名急性发热性疾病患者、3,484 只蚊子和 113 只非人类灵长类动物的样本。所有样本的登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒均为阴性。检测到一例输入性奥罗普切病毒感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
为了评估奥罗普切病毒、马亚罗病毒、西尼罗河病毒和圣路易斯脑炎病毒的传播情况,我们检测了来自巴西米纳斯吉拉斯州北部的 824 名急性发热性疾病患者、3,484 只蚊子和 113 只非人类灵长类动物的样本。
设计与对象
所有样本的登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒均为阴性。
核心方法
该研究测试了 2019 年至 2025 年期间巴西米纳斯吉拉斯州北部患者、蚊子和非人类灵长类动物的样本。
主要结果
检测到一例输入性奥罗普切病毒感染。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明该地区存在奥罗普切病毒,这可能对了解相关病毒的 流行病学 具有影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为 监测 在巴西米纳斯吉拉斯州北部针对奥罗普切病毒和相关虫媒病毒的努力提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其关注特定区域和时间范围而受到限制,并且证据基于单个检测到的奥罗普切病毒病例。

Molecular Surveillance for Oropouche, Mayaro, West Nile, and Saint Louis EncephalitisViruses, Brazil, 2019–2025

Authors: Alice S.N. Ferreira, Thaynara J. Teixeira, Danilo B. de Oliveira, Marco A.A. Schetino, Cirilo Henrique de Oliveira, Pedro A.A. Souza, Maysa F.A. Araújo, Thamires G. M. Silva, Arlei Bispo de Araújo, Ramon S. Oliveira
Original abstract (English)
To assess the circulation of Oropouche, Mayaro, West Nile, and St. Louis encephalitis viruses, we tested samples from 824 patients with acute febrile illness, 3,484 mosquitoes, and 113 nonhuman primates from northern Minas Gerais, Brazil. All samples were negative for dengue, Chikungunya, and Zika virus. One imported Oropouche virus infection was detected.

Question and background
To assess the circulation of Oropouche, Mayaro, West Nile, and St. Louis encephalitis viruses, we tested samples from 824 patients with acute febrile illness, 3,484 mosquitoes, and 113 nonhuman primates from northern Minas Gerais, Brazil.
Design and population
All samples were negative for dengue, Chikungunya, and Zika virus.

Main results
One imported Oropouche virus infection was detected.

The authors interpreted the findings as indicating the presence of Oropouche virus in the region, which may have implications for understanding the epidemiology of related viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance

CYD-TDV 疫苗接种生物统计特性:实施巴拉那-巴西州疫苗接种计划。

CARACTERIZAÇÃO BIOESTATÍSTICA DA EFICÁCIA DA VACINA CYD-TDV: IMPLEMENTAÇÃO NO PROGRAMA DE VACINAÇÃO DO ESTADO DO PARANÁ-BRASIL.
作者： Brunno Scudeller, Frederico Cardoso Buchalla
摘要中文翻译Vivemos em um país com 流行病频繁登革热,ano após ano。必须以最重要的方式、最重要的方式,作为传播和原则的形式,作为预防措施,不做任何事,努力解决社会代理人的问题,解决公共问题的问题,并为所有待办事项的人民提供响应噢,世界。比如说,蚊子是新病毒,是主要流行病,比如寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅热病毒。巴西国家领土流行病频繁发生。2015 年,寨卡病毒流行不断,登革热广泛流行,成为公共问题的难题,可能成为紧急情况说明和研究疫苗接种的有效性和安全性,以保护个人的健康。阿西姆是一个针对疫苗接种的紧急实验室,它是针对登革热病毒的有效研究,包括 4 种病毒,DENV-1、2、3、4,并在临床研究中取得了成功为疫苗接种提供有效和安全保障。此外,我们还对生物统计进行了长期研究,以期对 CYD-TDV 疫苗的功效和安全进行长期分析,并确定 CYD-TDV 疫苗的介绍。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
我们研究了 CYD-TDV 疫苗对抗登革热病毒的功效和安全性,并考虑到保护经常发生的流行病和新出现的寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅热病毒的必要性。
设计与对象
我们正在巴西地区实施 CYD-TDV 疫苗,并在巴拉那州实施疫苗接种计划,以分析登革热流行病和相关病毒的有效性和安全性。
核心方法
利用生物统计研究论坛来评估 CYD-TDV 疫苗的功效和安全性,对长期的疫苗接种进行分析,并考虑介绍疫苗、确定区域和流行病学的含义。
主要结果
生物统计证明了 CYD-TDV 疫苗的有效性和安全性,与登革热病毒有关,其结果是与 DENV-1、2、3 和 4 病毒相比具有显着的保护作用。
解释与创新
CYD-TDV 疫苗的功效解释是预防登革热的有效方法,我们对巴西特定地区的功效进行了分析。
科研与公卫意义
我们将向公共疫苗政策提供信息,并为流行病学的警戒做出贡献,特别是在登革热发生和病毒相关的地区。
局限与证据强度
生物统计研究的证据和基础,限制了对外部地区或民众的结果的概括,是对代表性和机密性的分析。

CARACTERIZAÇÃO BIOESTATÍSTICA DA EFICÁCIA DA VACINA CYD-TDV: IMPLEMENTAÇÃO NO PROGRAMA DE VACINAÇÃO DO ESTADO DO PARANÁ-BRASIL.

Authors: Brunno Scudeller, Frederico Cardoso Buchalla
Original abstract (English)
Vivemos em um país com epidemias frequentes de dengue, ano após ano. Muito se tem falado na mídia sobre essa doença, os sintomas, as formas de transmissão e principalmente, as maneiras de preveni-la, no entanto, apesar dos esforços dos agentes de saúde, a doença se tornou um problema de saúde pública, sendo responsável por milhares de pacientes em todo o mundo. Como se não bastasse, o mosquito adquiriu novos vírus, o que provocou uma das maiores epidemias, como a Zika e a Chikungunya. Desde então, as epidemias ocorreram frequentemente em todo o território nacional do Brasil.Dessa forma, após o surto de Zika, no ano de 2015, e as diversas epidemias sucessivas de Dengue, tornou-se um problema de saúde pública, o que possibilitou a urgência em descobrir e estudar uma vacina que estivesse ao alcance da eficácia e segurança necessárias para proteger o indivíduo dessa doença. Assim, a urgência dos laboratórios nas pesquisas desenvolvidas para a descoberta de uma vacina, que teria a eficácia comprovada para o vírus da dengue, em seus 4 sorotipos, DENV-1, 2, 3, 4, viabilizou uma possível falha em estudos clínicos que poderiam acarretar baixa eficácia e segurança para os pacientes submetidos a esse tipo de vacinação. Além disso, forneceu estudos bioestatísticos, que a longo prazo analisam a eficácia e segurança da vacina CYD-TDV em uma determinada região onde foi introduzida.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
O estudo busca compreender a eficácia e segurança da vacina CYD-TDV contra o vírus da dengue, considerando a necessidade de proteger a população diante de surtos frequentes e a emergência de novos vírus como a Zika e a Chikungunya.
Design and population
O estudo foi conduzido em uma região específica do Brasil, com a implementação da vacina CYD-TDV no programa de vacinação do Estado do Paraná, visando analisar sua eficácia e segurança em uma população exposta a epidemias de dengue e outros vírus relacionados.
Core methods
Foram utilizados estudos bioestatísticos para avaliar a eficácia e segurança da vacina CYD-TDV, com análise de dados coletados ao longo de um período prolongado, considerando a introdução da vacina em uma determinada região e suas implicações epidemiológicas.
Main results
Os estudos bioestatísticos demonstraram a eficácia e segurança da vacina CYD-TDV em relação ao vírus da dengue, com resultados que indicaram uma proteção significativa contra os sorotipos DENV-1, 2, 3 e 4.
Interpretation and novelty
A interpretação dos resultados sugere que a vacina CYD-TDV é uma ferramenta eficaz para a prevenção da dengue, com uma nova abordagem na análise de sua eficácia em uma região específica do Brasil.
Scientific and public-health significance
Os achados podem informar políticas públicas de vacinação e contribuir para a vigilância epidemiológica, especialmente em regiões com alta incidência de dengue e outros vírus relacionados.
Limitations and evidence strength
A evidência é baseada em estudos bioestatísticos, o que pode limitar a generalização dos resultados para outras regiões ou populações, mas a análise foi conduzida com uma amostra representativa e confiável.

基孔肯雅病毒 感染后慢性 关节痛 的预测因素:系统评价和荟萃分析。

Predictors of chronic arthralgia after chikungunya virus infection: a systematic review and meta-analysis.
作者： Lucas Bussiki Corrêa da Costa Kotecki, Vitória Diniz de Medeiros, Mariana Bussiki Corrêa da Costa Kotecki, Bianca Gabriela Tavares Castiglioni, Miguel Chaves Lenzi, Isadora Pasqualette Silva, Rafaela Coelho Duarte, João Vitor Kayser de Souza, Cláudia Figueredo Nascimento Salomão Santos, Gabriela Stutz F Moreira
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
基孔肯雅热是一种不断扩大的全球健康威胁,慢性 关节痛 是导致残疾的主要长期驱动因素。我们的目的是确定实验室确诊的基孔肯雅热后慢性 关节痛(≥3 个月)的可重复预测因子,并区分稳定的预后信号与背景依赖性关联。
证据基础与方法
我们检索了 PubMed、Embase、Cochrane Library 和 BVS/LILACS,从创建之初到 2026 年 2 月 6 日,没有任何限制。两名评审员独立筛选记录、提取数据并使用 QUIPS 评估偏倚风险。随机效应限制最大似然荟萃分析汇总了比值比和平均差异,并通过随访窗口进行亚组分析。普洛斯彼罗:CRD420251169320。
共识与结论
The most consistent associations were female sex [odds ratio (OR) 2.08, 95% confidence interval (CI) 1.77-2.46; I2 = 8.3%], comorbidity presence (1.99, 1.55-2.55; I2 = 0%) and high blood pressure (1.52, 1.13-2.04; I2 = 33.4%). Chronic post-chikungunya arthralgia was most reproducibly associated with readily identifiable baseline host factors, rather than with a uniform acute-severity phenotype.
争议与缺口
统一后,对 22 个预测因子进行了荟萃分析。相反,一些急性期表现和 治疗 相关标志物显示出不太稳定、时间依赖性或研究敏感的关联。大多数研究的总体偏倚风险较高,其余研究的总体偏倚风险为中等。
科研与实践启示
对具有这些特征的患者进行护理点识别可以支持 暴发 期间随访和资源分配的优先顺序,为 暴发 后的 暴发 和指南制定提供信息,并可以指导未来在流行病环境中基于负担的 暴发 策略。

Predictors of chronic arthralgia after chikungunya virus infection: a systematic review and meta-analysis.

Authors: Lucas Bussiki Corrêa da Costa Kotecki, Vitória Diniz de Medeiros, Mariana Bussiki Corrêa da Costa Kotecki, Bianca Gabriela Tavares Castiglioni, Miguel Chaves Lenzi, Isadora Pasqualette Silva, Rafaela Coelho Duarte, João Vitor Kayser de Souza, Cláudia Figueredo Nascimento Salomão Santos, Gabriela Stutz F Moreira
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Chikungunya is an expanding global health threat, with chronic arthralgia representing the main long-term driver of disability. Although many studies have assessed post-chikungunya outcomes, evidence remains fragmented and contradictory. We aimed to identify reproducible predictors of chronic arthralgia (≥3 months) after laboratory-confirmed chikungunya and to distinguish stable prognostic signals from context-dependent associations. METHODS: We searched PubMed, Embase, Cochrane Library and BVS/LILACS from inception to 6 February 2026, without restrictions. Eligible studies compared participants with and without chronic arthralgia at follow-up and reported extractable baseline or acute-phase factors. Two reviewers independently screened records, extracted data and assessed risk of bias using QUIPS. Random-effects restricted maximum likelihood meta-analyses pooled odds ratios and mean differences, with subgroup analyses by follow-up window. PROSPERO: CRD420251169320. RESULTS: Of 1784 records, 29 studies were included, comprising 4108 participants: 1878 with chronic arthralgia and 2230 without. After harmonization, 22 predictors were meta-analysed. The most consistent associations were female sex [odds ratio (OR) 2.08, 95% confidence interval (CI) 1.77-2.46; I2 = 8.3%], comorbidity presence (1.99, 1.55-2.55; I2 = 0%) and high blood pressure (1.52, 1.13-2.04; I2 = 33.4%). In contrast, several acute-phase manifestations and treatment-related markers showed less stable, timing-dependent or study-sensitive associations. Overall risk of bias was high in most studies and moderate in the remainder. CONCLUSIONS: Chronic post-chikungunya arthralgia was most reproducibly associated with readily identifiable baseline host factors, rather than with a uniform acute-severity phenotype. These findings suggest that risk stratification should not rely only on acute symptom burden, and that pre-existing vulnerability may define a clinically important high-risk group. Point-of-care identification of patients with these characteristics could support prioritization of follow-up and resource allocation during outbreaks, inform post-outbreak surveillance and guideline development, and may guide future burden-based vaccination strategies in endemic settings.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Chikungunya is an expanding global health threat, with chronic arthralgia representing the main long-term driver of disability. We aimed to identify reproducible predictors of chronic arthralgia (≥3 months) after laboratory-confirmed chikungunya and to distinguish stable prognostic signals from context-dependent associations.
Evidence base and method
We searched PubMed, Embase, Cochrane Library and BVS/LILACS from inception to 6 February 2026, without restrictions. Two reviewers independently screened records, extracted data and assessed risk of bias using QUIPS. Random-effects restricted maximum likelihood meta-analyses pooled odds ratios and mean differences, with subgroup analyses by follow-up window. PROSPERO: CRD420251169320.
Consensus and conclusions
The most consistent associations were female sex [odds ratio (OR) 2.08, 95% confidence interval (CI) 1.77-2.46; I2 = 8.3%], comorbidity presence (1.99, 1.55-2.55; I2 = 0%) and high blood pressure (1.52, 1.13-2.04; I2 = 33.4%). Chronic post-chikungunya arthralgia was most reproducibly associated with readily identifiable baseline host factors, rather than with a uniform acute-severity phenotype.
Controversies and gaps
After harmonization, 22 predictors were meta-analysed. In contrast, several acute-phase manifestations and treatment-related markers showed less stable, timing-dependent or study-sensitive associations. Overall risk of bias was high in most studies and moderate in the remainder.
Research and practice implications
Point-of-care identification of patients with these characteristics could support prioritization of follow-up and resource allocation during outbreaks, inform post-outbreak surveillance and guideline development, and may guide future burden-based vaccination strategies in endemic settings.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas Urbanas: revisión sistemática 城市地区登革热和基孔肯雅热的流行病学影响:系统评价

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática Epidemiological impact of dengue and chikungunya in urban areas: a systematic review
作者： Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Mité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
摘要中文翻译目的:证明城市流行病学对登革热和基孔肯雅热的影响。方法:PubMed 和 SciELO 中的 síntesis narrativa 的修订(2012-2025);包括 15 个工作室。结果:1990 年世界登革热事件,拉丁美洲地区市场和货运方面存在差异。基孔肯雅热在 2013 年美洲地区得到扩展,并产生了城市爆炸性的产品,并具有持久的关节表现。城市化、埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的扩张以及对传播有利于杀虫剂的抵抗力。Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado。结论:虫媒病毒在热带和亚热带城市中发挥着重要作用,并在预防和控制方面发挥着重要作用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
目的:证明城市流行病学对登革热和基孔肯雅热的影响。
证据基础与方法
方法:PubMed 和 SciELO 中的 síntesis narrativa 的修订(2012-2025);包括 15 个工作室。
共识与结论
结果:1990 年世界登革热事件,拉丁美洲地区市场和货运方面存在差异。基孔肯雅热在 2013 年美洲地区得到扩展,并产生了城市爆炸性的产品,并具有持久的关节表现。
争议与缺口
城市化、埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的扩张以及对传播有利于杀虫剂的抵抗力。
科研与实践启示
强调新的疫苗和综合媒介控制策略。

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática Epidemiological impact of dengue and chikungunya in urban areas: a systematic review

Authors: Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Mité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
Original abstract (English)
Objetivo: sintetizar la evidencia sobre el impacto epidemiológico urbano del dengue y del chikungunya. Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios. Resultados: la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013 y produjo brotes urbanos explosivos, con manifestaciones articulares persistentes. La urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus y la resistencia a insecticidas favorecieron la transmisión. Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado. Conclusión: ambas arbovirosis son una carga creciente para las ciudades tropicales y subtropicales y exigen vigilancia y control sostenidos.
View review five-element analysis

Evidence base and method
Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios.
Consensus and conclusions
Resultados: la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013 y produjo brotes urbanos explosivos, con manifestaciones articulares persistentes.
Controversies and gaps
La urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus y la resistencia a insecticidas favorecieron la transmisión.
Research and practice implications
Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado.
