印度儿童急性脑炎综合征的病因谱:系统评价和荟萃分析。

Etiological spectrum of acute encephalitis syndrome in children in India: A systematic review and meta-analysis.
作者： Prateek Kumar Panda, Indar Kumar Sharawat, Dibya Prasanna Rana, Arnav Ranjan, Siddhant Govekar, Harikrishnan Kodamana, Deepak Kumar, Kalpana Panda
摘要中文翻译背景:急性脑炎综合征(AES)仍然是印度儿童患病和死亡的重要原因。虽然日本脑炎(JE)病毒传统上被认为是主要原因,但最近的研究表明,致病微生物的范围已大大扩大。目的:估计印度 18 岁或以下儿童中由各种常见感染病因引起的 AES 病例的汇总比例。方法:对 PubMed/MEDLINE、EMBASE 和 Scopus 进行全面检索,查找 2000 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间发表的研究。在印度进行的研究,包括至少 25 名患有 AES 的儿童(≤ 18 岁),并报告了实验室确认的病因,被认为是合格的。病例报告 和小箱系列被排除在外。使用乔安娜布里格斯研究所清单对纳入的研究进行质量评估。使用随机效应模型来获得汇总估计值及其相应的 95% 置信区间 (CI)。结果:共有69项研究纳入最终分析。恙虫病是最常见的病因,占 AES 病例的 20%(95%CI:13%-27%),其次是乙脑,占 13%(95%CI:11%-15%)。肠道病毒占病例的 6%(95%CI:3%-9%)。登革热、单纯疱疹病毒和 Epstein-Barr 病毒各占病例的 5%(95%CI:分别为 3%-8%、2%-9% 和 1%-11%)。腮腺炎和 基孔肯雅病毒 各占 4%(95%CI:分别为 1%-8% 和 2%-5%)。结论:恙虫病和乙型脑炎是印度儿童 AES 的主要可识别病因。更广泛的诊断方法,包括分子技术,可以提高 AES 儿童的诊断率并促进早期靶向治疗。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是估计印度儿童中由各种常见感染病因引起的急性脑炎综合征 (AES) 病例的汇总比例,特别强调 基孔肯雅病毒。
证据基础与方法
对 2000 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间发表的研究进行了 PubMed/MEDLINE、EMBASE 和 Scopus 的全面检索。如果研究包括至少 25 名患有 AES 的儿童(≤ 18 岁)并报告了实验室确认的病因,则研究合格。病例报告 和小箱系列被排除在外。使用乔安娜布里格斯研究所清单对纳入的研究进行质量评估。使用随机效应模型来获得汇总估计值及其相应的 95% 置信区间 (CI)。
共识与结论
恙虫病和日本脑炎 (JE) 是印度儿科 AES 的主要可识别病因。基孔肯雅病毒 占病例的 4%(95%CI:2%-5%)。
争议与缺口
该综述强调,包括分子技术在内的更广泛的诊断方法可以提高 AES 儿童的诊断率并促进早期靶向治疗。然而,证据并未报告与 基孔肯雅病毒 相关的具体争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,改进的诊断方法(例如分子技术)可以增强对 基孔肯雅病毒 作为儿童 AES 病因的识别。这可能会带来更有针对性的治疗和干预措施。

Etiological spectrum of acute encephalitis syndrome in children in India: A systematic review and meta-analysis.

Authors: Prateek Kumar Panda, Indar Kumar Sharawat, Dibya Prasanna Rana, Arnav Ranjan, Siddhant Govekar, Harikrishnan Kodamana, Deepak Kumar, Kalpana Panda
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Acute encephalitis syndrome (AES) continues to be an important cause of illness and death among children in India. While Japanese encephalitis (JE) virus has traditionally been regarded as the leading cause, recent studies indicate that the range of causative organisms has widened considerably. AIM: To estimate the pooled proportion of AES cases attributable to various common infectious etiologies among children aged 18 years or younger in India. METHODS: A comprehensive search of PubMed/MEDLINE, EMBASE, and Scopus was conducted for studies published between January 2000 and December 2025. Studies conducted in India that included at least 25 children (≤ 18 years) with AES and reported laboratory-confirmed etiologies were considered eligible. Case reports and small case series were excluded. Quality assessment of included studies was performed using the Joanna Briggs Institute checklist. A random-effects model was used to obtain pooled estimates and their corresponding 95% confidence intervals (CI). RESULTS: A total of 69 studies were included in the final analysis. Scrub typhus emerged as the most common cause, accounting for 20% (95%CI: 13%-27%) of AES cases, followed by JE at 13% (95%CI: 11%-15%). Enterovirus accounted for 6% of the cases (95%CI: 3%-9%). Dengue, herpes simplex virus and Epstein-Barr virus accounted for 5% of the cases each (95%CI: 3%-8%, 2%-9%, and 1%-11%, respectively). Mumps and chikungunya virus accounted for 4% each (95%CI: 1%-8%, and 2%-5%, respectively). CONCLUSION: Scrub typhus and JE are the leading identifiable etiology of pediatric AES in India. Broader diagnostic methods, including molecular techniques, may improve the diagnostic yield and facilitate early targeted therapy in children with AES.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on estimating the pooled proportion of acute encephalitis syndrome (AES) cases in children in India attributable to various common infectious etiologies, with a specific emphasis on Chikungunya virus.
Evidence base and method
A comprehensive search of PubMed/MEDLINE, EMBASE, and Scopus was conducted for studies published between January 2000 and December 2025. Studies were eligible if they included at least 25 children (≤ 18 years) with AES and reported laboratory-confirmed etiologies. Case reports and small case series were excluded. Quality assessment of included studies was performed using the Joanna Briggs Institute checklist. A random-effects model was used to obtain pooled estimates and their corresponding 95% confidence intervals (CI).
Consensus and conclusions
Scrub typhus and Japanese encephalitis (JE) are the leading identifiable etiologies of pediatric AES in India. Chikungunya virus accounted for 4% of the cases (95%CI: 2%-5%).
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that broader diagnostic methods, including molecular techniques, may improve the diagnostic yield and facilitate early targeted therapy in children with AES. However, the evidence does not report specific controversies or evidence gaps related to Chikungunya virus.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that improved diagnostic methods, such as molecular techniques, could enhance the identification of Chikungunya virus as a cause of AES in children. This may lead to better-targeted therapies and interventions.

WHO Health Emergencies EPI-WIN webinar: Launch of WHO guidelines for clinical management of arboviral diseases: dengue, chikungunya, Zika and yellow fever - World Health Organization (WHO)

News Brief
The World Health Organization (WHO) launched guidelines for the clinical management of arboviral diseases, including chikungunya, dengue, Zika, and yellow fever, during a webinar on 4 November 2026. The guidelines aim to standardize treatment and care protocols for these diseases, which are prevalent in various regions globally. The event was reported by WHO as part of its efforts to enhance global health responses. The publication date of the news release was 2 November 2026, and the webinar was held in the Central European Time zone. The scale of impact is not quantified, but the initiative reflects WHO's ongoing commitment to addressing arboviral diseases through improved clinical practices. The response status indicates that the guidelines were officially launched, with no unresolved information or limitations mentioned in the source.

哥斯达黎加基孔肯雅热 暴发:针对游客的旅行健康警告 - ITIJ

Costa Rica chikungunya outbreak: travel health warning for tourists - ITIJ
新闻简报哥斯达黎加西北部地区,包括阿拉胡埃拉省和瓜纳卡斯特省,报告了基孔肯雅热 暴发,当地确诊为 传播。欧洲疾病预防控制中心 (ECDC) 指出,2026 年将出现 300 例病例,较 2025 年的 8 例大幅增加。暴发 已影响到外国游客,促使美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 发布 2 级旅行健康通知。卫生当局加强了蚊虫控制措施,英国外交、联邦和发展办公室 (FCDO) 建议旅行者确保为当地 治疗 和意外医疗后送提供适当的保险。症状包括发烧、严重关节疼痛、头痛和肌肉疼痛,关节问题可能会持续数月。我们鼓励旅客关注官方更新,在需要时寻求医疗建议,并在疾病影响他们的旅行时立即联系保险公司。这些警告并不一定意味着应该取消假期,但强烈建议采取预防措施。
查看新闻五要素
时间
哥斯达黎加基孔肯雅热 暴发:针对游客的旅行健康警告 随着哥斯达黎加西北部感染病例激增,游客面临新的健康风险,这可能会将梦想假期变成意想不到的医疗和保险挑战 由于该国西北部的基孔肯雅热 暴发 引发担忧,因此敦促前往哥斯达黎加的游客采取加强的健康预防措施。
地点与对象
Costa Rica,specifically the northwest region,including Alajuela and Guanacaste provinces,with a focus on the Pacific coast areas like Playa Langosta and Tamarindo.游客和外国游客是受影响的人群。
事件
哥斯达黎加西北部报告了基孔肯雅热 暴发,阿拉胡埃拉省和瓜纳卡斯特省发现了当地的 传播。欧洲疾病预防和控制中心 (ECDC) 报告 2026 年有 300 例病例,而 2025 年有 8 例。暴发 涉及外国游客中的感染。
规模、影响与风险
2026 年,暴发 已导致 300 例确诊病例,其中在特定省份发现了本地 传播。美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 已发布 2 级旅行健康通知,建议加强预防措施。这种疾病会导致发烧、严重关节疼痛、头痛和肌肉疼痛,关节症状可能会持续数月。卫生部加强了灭蚊措施。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生当局已确认涉及外国游客的感染病例,并正在实施蚊虫控制措施。CDC 维持 2 级旅行健康通知,英国 FCDO 建议为当地 治疗 和医疗后送提供适当的保险。敦促旅客关注官方更新并在必要时寻求医疗建议。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Costa Rica chikungunya outbreak: travel health warning for tourists - ITIJ

News Brief
A chikungunya outbreak has been reported in Costa Rica’s northwest region, including Alajuela and Guanacaste provinces, with local transmission confirmed. The European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control (ECDC) noted 300 cases in 2026, a significant increase from eight cases in 2025. The outbreak has affected foreign visitors, prompting the US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) to issue a level 2 travel health notice. Health authorities have intensified mosquito-control measures, and the UK Foreign, Commonwealth & Development Office (FCDO) advises travelers to ensure appropriate insurance for local treatment and unexpected medical evacuation. Symptoms include fever, severe joint pain, headaches, and muscle pain, with joint issues potentially lasting months. Travelers are encouraged to monitor official updates, seek medical advice when needed, and contact their insurer promptly if illness affects their trip. The warnings do not necessarily mean holidays should be canceled, but precautions are strongly advised.
Original source text (English)
Costa Rica chikungunya outbreak: travel health warning for tourists With infections surging in Costa Rica’s northwest, tourists face fresh health risks that could turn a dream holiday into an unexpected medical and insurance challenge Travellers visiting Costa Rica are being urged to take enhanced health precautions as a chikungunya outbreak in the country’s northwest raises concerns. The European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control (ECDC) has reported 300 cases in Costa Rica in 2026, with local transmission identified in Alajuela and Guanacaste provinces. This compares with eight cases reported in 2025. The US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) maintains a level 2 travel health notice, advising visitors to practise enhanced precautions. The outbreak is particularly relevant to holidaymakers visiting Guanacaste’s Pacific coast, including Playa Langosta and the popular Tamarindo area. Costa Rica’s Ministry of Health has confirmed infections involving foreign visitors and has intensified mosquito-control measures. Chikungunya is transmitted through infected mosquito bites and can cause fever, severe joint pain, headaches, and muscle pain. Although many patients recover within days, joint symptoms can persist for months or longer, potentially affecting planned excursions and return journeys. The UK Foreign, Commonwealth & Development Office (FCDO) advises travellers to ensure they have appropriate insurance for local treatment and unexpected medical evacuation. Health authorities recommend effective insect repellent, long-sleeved clothing, and screened or air-conditioned accommodation. Eligible travellers should consult a healthcare professional about vaccination before departure. The warnings do not automatically mean holidays should be cancelled. However, travellers should monitor official updates, seek medical advice when appropriate, and contact their insurer promptly if illness affects their trip. She writes for ITIJ and AirMed&Rescue, covering a range of topics including international travel and health insurance, medical assistance provision, and air medical transportation. Chloe holds a BA (Hons) in English and an MA in English Literature from the University of Bristol

Réunion’s Monitored Chikungunya Epidemic Offers Preparedness Lessons for Europe - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报本文讨论了 2024 年至 2025 年留尼汪岛的基孔肯雅热疫情,强调了其记录在案的影响以及对欧洲公共卫生准备的教训。2024年8月23日发现第一例本土病例,疫情于2025年6月27日正式结束,但低水平循环仍在继续。暴发 导致 54,250 例实验室确诊病例、195,800 次初级保健咨询和近 3,000 次急诊科就诊。初步数据显示,有 1,200 人住院治疗,45 人死亡。《公共卫生档案》评论的作者指出,该流行病的监测系统(包括病毒学监测系统和 媒介 监测 系统)十分健全,但在操作响应方面却暴露出差距。他们强调初级保健需要预先确定的策略和激增的能力。一项针对老年人的免费 疫苗接种 活动于 2025 年 4 月开始,但出于安全考虑而停止,该活动已报告 47 起不良事件病例,其中 18 例严重病例和 3 例死亡。作者警告不要将留尼汪岛视为欧洲大陆的直接模式,理由是人口免疫力、地理和医疗保健系统存在差异。

时间
疫情始于2024年8月23日发现的第一例本土病例,官方疫情阶段于2025年6月27日结束。文章发表日期为2026年11月3日。
地点与对象
留尼汪岛,法国。疫情影响了整个 population,疫情后血清学调查估计血清流行率为 66.0 percent,症状性攻击率约为 44 percent。
事件
2024年至2025年,留尼汪岛爆发基孔肯雅热疫情,实验室​​确诊病例54,250例,初级保健咨询195,800人次,急诊科就诊近3,000人次。《公共卫生档案》评论的作者分析了该流行病的影响以及公共卫生准备的教训。
规模、影响与风险
此次疫情导致确诊病例54,250例,基孔肯雅热相关初级保健咨询195,800人次,急诊就诊近3,000人次。初步数据显示,约有 1,200 人住院治疗,45 人死亡。作者指出,该流行病的规模和相关的医疗负担凸显了初级保健激增能力的必要性。
应对、状态与不确定性
疫情于2025年6月27日正式宣布结束,但低水平的病毒传播依然存在。免费的 疫苗接种 活动于 2025 年 4 月 7 日开始,但出于安全考虑,该活动于 2025 年 4 月 26 日暂停向老年人提供。作者强调了流行病前规划对 疫苗 部署和药物警戒的重要性。

Réunion’s Monitored Chikungunya Epidemic Offers Preparedness Lessons for Europe - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
The article discusses the 2024–2025 chikungunya epidemic on Réunion Island, highlighting its documented impact and lessons for public health preparedness in Europe. The first autochthonous cases were detected on 23 August 2024, and the epidemic officially ended on 27 June 2025, though low-level circulation continued. The outbreak led to 54,250 laboratory-confirmed cases, 195,800 primary-care consultations, and nearly 3,000 emergency-department visits. Preliminary data suggested 1,200 hospitalizations and 45 deaths. The authors of a Commentary in Archives of Public Health note that the epidemic's monitoring systems, including virological and vector surveillance, were robust but revealed gaps in operational response. They emphasize the need for pre-defined strategies and surge capacity in primary care. A free vaccination campaign started in April 2025 but was halted for older adults due to safety concerns, with 47 adverse-event cases reported, including 18 serious cases and three deaths. The authors caution against treating Réunion as a direct model for mainland Europe, citing differences in population immunity, geography, and healthcare systems.

HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Tuesday, November 3, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Réunion’s Monitored Chikungunya Epidemic Offers Preparedness Lessons for Europe by October 30, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 5 mins read 0 Réunion's Monitored Chikungunya Epidemic Offers Preparedness Lessons for Europe Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram The 2024–25 chikungunya epidemic on Réunion Island has become an unusually well-documented case study in how difficult it can be to turn early warning into timely public-health action during a fast-moving arboviral outbreak. In a Commentary published in Archives of Public Health, a team of French infectious-diseases and public-health researchers argues that the island’s experience, despite some of the most intensive outbreak surveillance anywhere in the region, exposes gaps that other territories increasingly exposed to mosquito-borne viruses cannot afford to ignore. The authors draw three central lessons from the epidemic: the challenge of translating surveillance signals into operational response, the pressure that surging case numbers placed on primary care, and the complexity of deploying a newly authorized vaccine in the middle of an ongoing epidemic. The chronology of the outbreak is central to the analysis. The first autochthonous chikungunya cases, meaning locally acquired infections rather than imported ones, were detected on 23 August 2024. Local transmission then progressively intensified, and Réunion officially entered the epidemic phase on 13 January 2025. After several months of sustained transmission, declining epidemiological indicators led the prefect of Réunion, on the recommendation of the Regional Health Agency (ARS), to return the ORSEC arbovirus response plan to level 2 on 27 June 2025, formally marking the end of the epidemic phase. Even then, low-level viral circulation persisted on the island after the official phase had ended. Discover more infection Get Health Tips health The burden of the epidemic was substantial. It resulted in 54,250 laboratory-confirmed cases, an estimated 195,800 chikungunya-related primary-care consultations, and nearly 3,000 emergency-department visits. Preliminary hospital and Santé publique France surveillance data, which the authors note are still being consolidated, indicate approximately 1,200 hospitalizations and 45 deaths. A post-epidemic serosurvey estimated overall chikungunya virus seroprevalence at 66.0 percent, of which approximately 46 percentage points were estimated to be attributable to the 2025 epidemic after accounting for residual immunity from the 2005–2006 epidemic that also struck the island. A population-weighted survey conducted at the end of the epidemic estimated a symptomatic attack rate of approximately 44 percent, with marked geographical heterogeneity across the island. Discover more Check Symptoms Track Outbreaks Donate to Research public-health The serosurvey also addressed a question that often arises during epidemics: whether some age groups are disproportionately infected. Although recent infection varied across age groups in unadjusted analyses, age was not independently associated with infection after adjustment. In other words, once other factors were accounted for, being older or younger did not by itself determine the likelihood of infection. This distinction between crude, unadjusted patterns and adjusted analytical findings is one the authors take care to preserve, and it matters for anyone interpreting who was actually at greatest risk of acquiring the virus during this outbreak. What made Réunion unusual was not only the size of the epidemic but the density of monitoring surrounding it. The outbreak was tracked through complementary systems combining laboratory-confirmed case reporting, primary-care and hospital surveillance, virological characterization of the circulating virus, vector surveillance, serological investigations and, once vaccination began, pharmacovigilance. Epidemiological and healthcare indicators were monitored in near real time during the epidemic, while population-level immunity was assessed afterwards through a dedicated post-epidemic serosurvey. This layered architecture allowed early detection of autochthonous transmission in August 2024, rapid characterization of the circulating chikungunya virus lineage, and close tracking of epidemic dynamics as they unfolded. Yet the first lesson the authors draw is a sobering one about the limits of detection. Integrated epidemiological and virological surveillance worked as designed, identifying local transmission early and characterizing the virus quickly. However, the subsequent acceleration of transmission illustrates that interventions requiring substantial operational preparation, including vaccination, may be difficult to deploy rapidly once epidemic growth has begun. The authors are careful about what this does and does not show: rather than demonstrating a generalized delay in the response, the epidemic timeline highlights the importance of defining response strategies, decision thresholds and operational mechanisms before epidemic escalation, so that they can be activated rather than constructed while case numbers climb. The second lesson concerns healthcare capacity. Around the epidemic peak, chikungunya-related consultations accounted for 18 percent of total general-practitioner activity, with an estimated 20,520 consultations for clinically compatible chikungunya cases in epidemiological week 15 alone. The authors note that the effect of this increased demand on access to care for other conditions was not quantitatively assessed, an important limitation, but they argue that these observations nonetheless demonstrate why primary-care surge capacity must be incorporated into arbovirus preparedness plans. Discover more Public Health public health Study Biology infectious-diseases infections The third lesson is the most cautionary. The free IXCHIQ vaccination campaign began on 7 April 2025, initially targeting individuals at highest risk of severe disease, including adults aged 65 years and older, particularly those with comorbidities. Approximately 6,400 doses had been administered by late April. Previous acceptability data from Réunion had shown that willingness to receive chikungunya vaccination was strongly context-dependent, particularly on whether vaccination was provided free of charge, a finding the authors cite to underline the importance of affordability and delivery conditions when planning emergency vaccine deployment. The safety trajectory of the campaign then unfolded quickly. Following reports of serious adverse events after immunization, French health authorities recommended on 26 April that IXCHIQ should no longer be administered to persons aged 65 years and older. Enhanced pharmacovigilance subsequently identified 47 reported adverse-event cases, including 18 serious cases and three deaths. Pharmacological assessment supported an association with IXCHIQ in 9 of the 18 serious cases, while an association was considered very likely for one of the three deaths. Similar safety concerns, particularly serious neurological and cardiac events in older adults, were also identified through post-authorization surveillance in the United States. For the authors, these events illustrate the importance of anticipating pharmacovigilance, benefit–risk reassessment and communication strategies before, not after, a newly authorized vaccine is deployed during an epidemic. The Commentary is explicit that Réunion should not be treated as a direct epidemiological model for mainland Europe. The island combines established Aedes albopictus mosquito populations with tropical climatic conditions that can sustain transmission over a prolonged period, whereas chikungunya transmission in mainland Europe remains strongly seasonal and dependent on the introduction of viraemic cases into areas where competent vectors are active. Population immunity, geography and healthcare organization also differ substantially between settings. Nevertheless, the authors argue that the operational lessons travel well: translating surveillance signals into predefined response thresholds, anticipating healthcare surge capacity, preparing vaccine deployment and pharmacovigilance mechanisms in advance, ensuring rapid data sharing, and embedding research within outbreak response. These preparedness principles may be relevant beyond Réunion, but their implementation must be adapted to local epidemiological and health-system contexts. The conclusions the authors reach are deliberately operational rather than dramatic. Effective preparedness, they write, requires more than early detection. Surveillance signals need to be linked to predefined escalation thresholds and operational response mechanisms; primary-care and hospital surge capacity should be anticipated; and vaccine-deployment strategies, including logistics, pharmacovigilance, benefit–risk reassessment and communication, should be established before epidemic acceleration. Timely data sharing across epidemiological, virological and healthcare surveillance systems is essential, while clinical and vaccine-effectiveness studies should be designed for rapid activation and embedded within the outbreak response itself. For regions increasingly exposed to chikungunya, the Réunion experience suggests that preparedness depends not only on detecting the next outbreak early, but on having the mechanisms in place to act on that information rapidly. As chikungunya transmission now occurs in new areas of Europe, the island stands as a sentinel at the crossroads of intense regional mobility, climate-driven vector expansion and increasing arboviral emergence. A chikungunya epidemic occurred on Réunion Island from 2024 to 2025, with 54,250 laboratory-confirmed cases, 195,800 primary-care consultations, and nearly 3,000 emergency-department visits. 暴发自2024年8月以来一直持续,2025年初至2025年6月25日报告病例,2025年6月第一周(流行病学第23周)显着增加。

Chikungunya Expansion in the Greater Metropolitan Area of Costa Rica - Atlas Global de Riesgo

News Brief
The Chikungunya virus has been reported in multiple locations, including Mainland France, Réunion Island, and Ecuador. In Mainland France, eight locally acquired cases were reported from the beginning of 2025 through June 25, 2025, with 223 cases identified since late January, 29 requiring hospitalization, and one death reported in an unvaccinated school-aged child. In Réunion Island, 1,766 new cases were reported last week, an 18% increase compared to the previous week. In Ecuador, 771 cases have been reported nationwide, the highest in the past five years, with Guayas Province leading in infections. The outbreak has been ongoing since August 2024, and several regions remain under alert status. Investigations are ongoing, and the situation is being monitored for potential increases in cases.

Time
The outbreak has been ongoing since August 2024, with cases reported from the beginning of 2025 through June 25, 2025, and a significant increase in the first week of June (epidemiological week 23) of 2025.
Location and population
The cases are distributed across six regions in France: Hérault, Var, Gard, Drôme, Bouches-du-Rhône, and Corse-du-Sud. Additionally, the outbreak is noted in Réunion Island (France) and Guayas Province, Zamora Chinchipe, and Loja in Ecuador.

A total of 771 cases have been reported nationwide in Ecuador, the highest in the past five years. 新框架在微博上追踪基孔肯雅热 暴发 期间的公众舆论变化 - Bioengineer.org

New framework tracks public opinion shifts during a Chikungunya outbreak on Weibo - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报A new framework, introduced by researchers at Bioengineer.org, tracks public opinion shifts during a Chikungunya fever outbreak on Weibo. The framework couples event knowledge graphs with a topic modeling approach, using a Centroid-Alignment Factor (C-AF) embedding-enhanced BERTopic model. It was applied to 22,744 Weibo posts related to the outbreak, improving Topic Diversity from 0.5725 to 0.6480 and achieving a peak Silhouette Coefficient of 0.909, which outperforms the baseline by 35.7 percent. The study, published on 9 October 2026, identifies five distinct user profiles based on their posting behavior during the outbreak. However, the framework's performance on other platforms, languages, and public health events remains untested. The research is open access and aims to provide a scalable, data-driven tool for public health crisis monitoring and targeted digital governance intervention.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该框架是在有关 暴发 的微博帖子的单个数据集上开发和测试的,该文章发表于 2026 年 10 月 9 日。暴发 的跟踪截止日期为 2026 年 10 月 28 日。
地点与对象
该框架适用于与中国社交媒体平台 基孔肯雅热 暴发 相关的微博帖子。
事件
引入了一个新框架,使用事件知识图和主题建模来跟踪基孔肯雅热 暴发 期间的舆论变化。该框架是在与 暴发 相关的微博帖子的单个数据集上开发和测试的。
规模、影响与风险
该框架将主题多样性从0.5725提升至0.6480,并实现了0.909的峰值轮廓系数,比基线方法提高了35.7个百分点。这些指标描述的是模型在语料库上的性能,而非具体领域的结果。
应对、状态与不确定性
该框架是在单个数据集上开发和测试的,其在其他平台、语言和公共卫生事件上的性能仍有待证明。该研究作为开放获取研究发表在 EPJ Data Science 上。

New framework tracks public opinion shifts during a Chikungunya outbreak on Weibo - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A new framework, introduced by researchers at Bioengineer.org, tracks public opinion shifts during a Chikungunya fever outbreak on Weibo. The framework couples event knowledge graphs with a topic modeling approach, using a Centroid-Alignment Factor (C-AF) embedding-enhanced BERTopic model. It was applied to 22,744 Weibo posts related to the outbreak, improving Topic Diversity from 0.5725 to 0.6480 and achieving a peak Silhouette Coefficient of 0.909, which outperforms the baseline by 35.7 percent. The study, published on 9 October 2026, identifies five distinct user profiles based on their posting behavior during the outbreak. However, the framework's performance on other platforms, languages, and public health events remains untested. The research is open access and aims to provide a scalable, data-driven tool for public health crisis monitoring and targeted digital governance intervention.

HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Tuesday, November 3, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Technology New framework tracks public opinion shifts during a Chikungunya outbreak on Weibo by October 28, 2026 in Technology Reading Time: 3 mins read 0 New framework tracks public opinion shifts during a Chikungunya outbreak on Weibo Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Researchers have introduced a dual-level framework that couples event knowledge graphs with a topic modeling approach to follow how public opinion evolves on social media during public health emergencies. The work, published in EPJ Data Science, was applied to 22,744 Weibo posts related to the Chikungunya fever outbreak. The framework addresses two persistent problems in social media monitoring: the semantic noise that arises when analyzing very short texts, and the fragmented way in which discourse structures and user behavior have typically been studied separately. The authors combine Event Knowledge Graphs (EKG) with user behavior profiling, both built on a Centroid-Alignment Factor (C-AF) embedding-enhanced BERTopic model. When applied to the Weibo corpus, the C-AF model improved Topic Diversity from 0.5725 to 0.6480. According to the study, this optimization enabled the construction of a high-precision event knowledge graph that reveals core discourse evolutionary pathways, showing how conversation themes shifted as the outbreak unfolded. Discover more Check Symptoms Track Outbreaks public health Public Health For user behavior segmentation, the framework achieved a peak Silhouette Coefficient of 0.909, which the authors report as outperforming the 0.670 baseline by 35.7 percent. An external semantic indicator, Topic Coherence, also improved from 0.550 to 0.783, providing a second line of validation for the clustering results. This dual validation allowed the researchers to identify five distinct user profiles among the accounts posting about the outbreak: Early Cognitive, Knowledge Dissemination, Risk-Attention, Prevention-Focused, and Preventive Alert types. Each profile represents a different pattern of information behavior, from early awareness of the event to active dissemination of knowledge and attention to preventive measures. The authors position the framework as a scalable, data-driven tool for precise public health crisis monitoring and targeted digital governance intervention. By linking what people are talking about with how different groups of users behave, the approach aims to give authorities a more granular picture of public sentiment during an emergency than topic modeling alone can provide. The study is published as open access research in EPJ Data Science, with the article shared early to provide faster access to peer-reviewed, accepted research; the published version is subject to further edits and will be replaced by the final Version of Record. The article was published on 9 October 2026 and is authored by Juan Wang, Xianghui Kong and Xiaojing Zhang. Discover more Try Microblogs opinion Read News Blogs Knowledge Graphs knowledge graphs Because the framework was developed and tested on a single dataset of Weibo posts about one outbreak, its performance on other platforms, languages, and public health events remains to be demonstrated. The reported metrics describe model performance on this corpus rather than outcomes of any intervention deployed in the field, and the identified user profiles characterize posting behavior within this specific dataset. Subject of Research: A topic modeling and event knowledge graph framework for tracking public opinion and user behavior on social media during a public health emergency. The framework was applied to Weibo posts related to the Chikungunya fever outbreak, which is a social media platform in China.
Event
A new framework was introduced to track public opinion shifts during a Chikungunya outbreak using event knowledge graphs and topic modeling. The framework was developed and tested on a single dataset of Weibo posts related to the outbreak.
Scale, impact and risk
The framework improved Topic Diversity from 0.5725 to 0.6480 and achieved a peak Silhouette Coefficient of 0.909, outperforming the baseline by 35.7 percent. These metrics describe model performance on the corpus rather than field outcomes.
Response, status and uncertainty
The framework was developed and tested on a single dataset, and its performance on other platforms, languages, and public health events remains to be demonstrated. The study is published as open access research in EPJ Data Science.

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
登革热VS基孔肯雅热:症状对比、传播途径和预防措施
News Brief
The supplied evidence does not clearly report the event time in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly identify the location or affected population. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the central event in verified English. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the scale, impact, or risk in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly report the response status or unresolved information.
Original source text (Chinese)
所谓“蚊媒传染病”,就是蚊子在叮咬时把病毒或寄生虫“快递”到人体内的疾病;国内需要重点盯防的有登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒病、乙型脑炎和疟疾等。 今天,我们先锁定由伊蚊 “ 投递 ” 的两大热门 疾病 —— 登革热与基孔肯雅热。 图源:云南疾控 0 1 什么是登革热? 它是由登革病毒引起的,经伊蚊叮咬传播的虫媒传染病。据世界卫生组织统计,全球每年约有1-4亿人感染,其中美洲、东南亚和西太平洋区域发病最为严重,亚洲约占全球疾病负担的70%。我国广东、云南、广西等多个省份曾多次发生本地疫情。 展开全文 0 2 登革热的典型症状有哪些? 登革热的潜伏期(从被携带病毒的蚊子叮咬到症状开始的时间)一般为1-14天,多为5-9天。值得注意的是大多数感染者没有症状或症状轻微,不过,一旦发病,患者会出现一系列典型症状: 1.突发高热: 体温可达40°C以上; 2.“三痛”: 剧烈头痛、眼眶痛、全身肌肉关节痛; 3.“三红”: 面部、颈部、胸部潮红; 4.皮疹: 四肢或躯干出现充血性红疹; 5.消化道症状: 恶心、呕吐、腹泻、食欲不振等消化道症状; 6.严重者: 可发展为登革出血热或登革休克综合征,甚至危及生命。 0 3 什么是基孔肯雅热? 基孔肯雅热(Chikungunya fever)是一种由基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)引起,通过白纹伊蚊或埃及伊蚊传播的病毒性疾病。 基孔肯雅热始发于非洲,最早于1952年在坦桑尼亚首次被发现。 “基孔肯雅”一词就来源于坦桑尼亚南部 基马孔德语 ,意思是“变得扭曲”,用以描述出现严重关节疼痛的感染者痛得弯腰、蜷缩身体的样子。 根据世界卫生组织公布的最新数据,截至2024年12月,全球119个国家和地区已报告基孔肯雅热本地传播。 近些年我国周边的东南亚疫情持续活跃。我国于2008年首次发现输入性病例,曾引发本地疫情,但尚未形成稳定的疫源地。2025年7月广东省佛山市发生疫情,经全力防控,现疫情已呈明显下降趋势。 0 4 基孔肯雅热有何症状? 被带 基孔肯雅 病毒的蚊子叮咬后,病毒在体内潜伏期通常为3-7天(最短1天,最长可达12天),随后可能突然发病,出现典型的“热-疹-痛”三联征: 🔺 突发高烧: 体温可高达39°C以上,可能持续1-7天。部分患者会出现“双峰热”,即退烧后再次发热。 🔺 皮疹: 约80%的患者在发热后2-5天,面部、四肢甚至手掌脚底会出现红色斑疹或丘疹,常伴有瘙痒。 🔺 明显关节疼痛: 这是基孔肯雅热最突出的特点之一。特别是手指、手腕、脚踝和脚趾等小关节会出现剧烈疼痛和肿胀,可能持续数天甚至数月。 此外,还可能伴随头痛、恶心、呕吐、肌肉痛、结膜炎等症状。 0 5 这两种疾病有什么区别? 这两种疾病,各年龄人群普遍易感,发病初期都会出现高热、头痛、关节和肌肉疼痛等症状。 登革热发生重症的比例较高, 致死病例时有发生。 基孔肯雅热多数患者为轻症 ,大约会在1~2周内康复,但部分人(尤其是老年人和慢性病患者)可能会经历长期关节疼痛的折磨;只有极少数情况下,该病会导致出血、脑炎等严重并发症甚至死亡。 两种疾病的特征对比 在登革热和基孔肯雅热共同流行的地区,常有人同时感染两种病毒。 0 6 这两种疾病是如何传播的? 登革热和基孔肯雅热的传播途径相似,都是由白纹伊蚊和埃及伊蚊传播。伊蚊白天叮咬人,尤其活跃于日出后1-2小时和日落前2-3小时。它们的幼虫孳生于小型积水容器中,如瓶罐、废旧轮胎、花盆等,这些地方都可能成为它们的“温床”。 0 7 如何预防这两种疾病? 面对蚊媒传染病的威胁,我们可以通过科学有效的预防措施,降低被其感染的风险: 1.环境治理: 伊蚊依赖小型积水繁殖,因此清除蚊虫孳生地是预防虫媒传染病的根本措施。我们需要定时清理家中容易积水的地方,如瓶罐、废旧轮胎、花盆、下水道、杂物堆等区域,不给蚊子提供繁殖的机会。 2.个人防护: 外出时,尽量穿浅色长袖衣裤,减少皮肤暴露面积;同时在裸露皮肤上涂抹含避蚊胺、避蚊酯的驱蚊液。在家中,安装纱门纱窗,使用蚊帐,为自己打造一个安全的“无蚊空间”;必要时使用电蚊拍、灭蚊灯等,对蚊子进行“精准打击”。此外,尽量减少在户外树阴、草丛等蚊子多的地方逗留时间。 返回搜狐,查看更多
